Motivation: VLMs在处理和学习密集连续视觉历史上存在瓶颈，阻碍单次尝试内的即时错误修正(试内)和多次尝试间的经验提取(试间)。问题的根源在于GUI导航中的信息不对称：预期转换可压缩为轻量文本摘要，但意外结果需要保留截图作为因果证据。

Method: 提出认知状态锚点(CSA)作为每步原语，主动比较预期与观察到的转换，将被动多模态轨迹转换为显式预测误差信号。这些信号通过非对称记忆驱动双尺度学习机制：试内修正使用滑动窗口选择性保留检测到不匹配的视觉证据；试间蒸馏将此非对称记忆聚焦于可能失败步骤的计算昂贵信用分配搜索空间。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上，AnchorGUI达到57.3%成功率，每步令牌减少2.4倍。跨试蒸馏达到69.2%成功率(+11.9%增益)，显著优于标准反射方法，同时保持亚线性上下文扩展。

Conclusion: AnchorGUI通过CSA机制有效解决了VLM GUI导航中的视觉历史处理瓶颈问题，证明了信息不对称假设在视觉-语言导航任务中的重要价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable autonomous GUI navigation, but agents still struggle to process and learn from dense, continuous visual histories. This bottleneck hinders both immediate error correction within a single episode (intra-trial) and experience distillation across multiple attempts (cross-trial). We trace these challenges to an empirical informational asymmetry in GUI navigation: while expected transitions can often be compressed into lightweight textual summaries, unexpected outcomes benefit from preserved screenshots as causal evidence for accurate diagnosis. Building on this insight, we propose AnchorGUI, a unified framework driven by the Cognitive State Anchor (CSA). The CSA acts as a per-step primitive that actively compares expected and observed transitions, converting passive multimodal trajectories into explicit prediction-error signals. These signals orchestrate a dual-scale learning mechanism via an asymmetric memory. For intra-trial correction, a sliding window selectively retains visual evidence for detected mismatches, providing immediate, visually-grounded feedback. For cross-trial distillation, this asymmetric memory focuses the computationally expensive credit assignment search space on likely failure steps. Experiments across four benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our approach. On AndroidWorld, AnchorGUI achieves a 57.3% success rate with a $2.4\times$ token reduction per step. Furthermore, cross-trial distillation reaches 69.2% success (+11.9% gain), significantly outperforming standard reflection methods while maintaining sub-linear context scaling.

</details>


### [173] [BVB: Benchmarking Agentic Video Understanding via Programmatic Reconstruction in Blender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15478)
*Yolo Y. Tang,Daiki Shimada,Jiayue Meng,Jing Bi,Pinxin Liu,Yicheng Wang,Yunzhong Xiao,Zhangyun Tan,Zeliang Zhang,Chao Huang,Susan Liang,Qianxiang Shen,Luchuan Song,Ali Vosoughi,Mingqian Feng,Melika Filvantorkaman,Chenliang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BVB是一个评估多模态智能体视频理解能力的基准测试，通过要求智能体用Blender编程重建视频来测试理解程度，优于传统问答评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准主要通过问答评估模型，但真正的视频理解应该能够程序化重建视频。论文提出如果智能体真正理解视频，应能通过编程重建它。

Method: 引入Blender-VideoBench(BVB)基准，要求智能体重建真实世界视频为Blender动画场景。通过Mini-BVB在相同沙箱中编程，设置共享成本限制。评估两个维度：(1) Dual VQA测量保留的时空事实数量；(2) Latent Similarity测量重建与源视频的感知相似度。使用均方根作为总体评分。

Result: 评估了10个模型家族的51种配置。最佳模型达到88.6 Latent Similarity，但仅保留53.7%的源正确时空答案。额外推理提高视觉相似性但未缩小事实准确性差距。15名评分者和5个配置的盲测显示Latent Similarity与人类偏好强相关。

Conclusion: 程序化重建是智能体视频理解的有效测试方法，语义保留仍是主要挑战。

Abstract: Multimodal agents can create complex videos in software such as Blender by coding without relying on diffusion models. Yet video understanding benchmarks still evaluate models mainly through question answering. If an agent truly understands a video, it can reconstruct it programmatically. We introduce BVB, Blender-VideoBench, a benchmark that tests this ability by asking agents to reconstruct real-world videos as animated Blender scenes. To ensure fair comparison, each agent programs the reconstruction through a lightweight harness, Mini-BVB, in an identical sandbox under a shared cost limit. The benchmark renders each reconstruction from its animated camera and evaluates it on two axes: (1) Dual VQA measures how many spatiotemporal facts the reconstruction preserves. (2) Latent Similarity measures how closely the reconstruction matches the source video perceptually. Our overall score, a square-root mean, favors balanced performance. We evaluate 51 configurations from 10 model families and analyze semantic retention, perceptual similarity, reasoning effort, and cost. The best model reaches 88.6 Latent Similarity but retains only 53.7% of the source-correct spatiotemporal answers. Additional reasoning improves visual similarity but does not close this gap in factual accuracy. In a blind study with 15 raters and five configurations, Latent Similarity correlates strongly with human preference. These results show that programmatic reconstruction is a viable test of agentic video understanding, and that semantic retention remains the main challenge.

</details>


### [174] [CatchMonitor: a machine learning system for automated fish discard quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15484)
*Geoff French,Michal Mackiewicz,Mark Fisher,Helen Holah,Rebecca Lamb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CatchMonitor系统的持续开发，这是一个用于自动量化拖网渔船视频中丢弃鱼类的计算机视觉原型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析拖网渔船监控视频是一个具有挑战性的问题，因为渔船上的真实世界条件复杂。需要开发自动化的丢弃鱼类量化系统来替代人工分析。

Method: 1. 应用半监督学习提高物种识别的准确性；2. 采用简单稳健的目标跟踪方法构建丢弃量化系统；3. 使用多个专家人工分析师的量化结果作为基准来评估系统性能。

Result: 系统能够自动从REM系统收集的视频中量化丢弃鱼类，半监督学习提高了物种识别准确率，专家分析师的标注变异性为系统性能评估提供了基准。

Conclusion: 通过结合半监督学习和稳健的目标跟踪方法，CatchMonitor原型系统能够有效自动量化拖网渔船上的丢弃鱼类，其性能可与人类专家相媲美。

Abstract: We report on the continued development of CatchMonitor, resulting in a prototype computer vision system designed to automatically quantify discarded fish from video footage collected from Remote Electronic Monitoring (REM) systems on fishing trawlers. The analysis of trawler surveillance footage is a challenging problem due to the real-world conditions on board fishing vessels. Building on our prior work we improve the accuracy of species identification through the application of semi-supervised learning. We utilise a simple and robust object tracking approach, upon which we build our prototype discard quantification system. Finally we analyse the variability of manual discard quantification performed by multiple expert human analysts, using it as a benchmark against which we compare the performance of our system.

</details>


### [175] [SyntheticDoc: A Large Synthetic Dataset for Document Unwarping and Illumination Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15503)
*Daniel Woortmann,Tanguy Magne,Olga Sorkine-Hornung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SyntheticDoc，一个大规模、高质量的文档去扭曲训练数据集，包含100万个高分辨率的程序生成样本，旨在解决文档纠正和光照校正任务中训练数据不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在文档纠正和光照校正方面表现受限于训练数据质量，近十年来社区主要依赖Doc3D数据集，但其规模和质量问题日益凸显，已成为该领域发展的瓶颈。

Method: 使用物理模拟器生成纸张几何结构，通过路径追踪器进行渲染，生成了包含UV贴图、法线贴图、反照率和着色等精确标注的百万级训练样本。

Result: 在文档去扭曲和光照校正任务上，使用SyntheticDoc训练的简单基线模型即可达到与当前最先进方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: SyntheticDoc数据集能够有效提升文档纠正模型的性能，其大规模和高标注质量为该领域的研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become the standard tool for document rectification and illumination correction, yet their performance is fundamentally bound by their training data. For nearly a decade, the community has heavily relied on Doc3D, a pioneering but increasingly limited document unwarping dataset in terms of scale and quality. To address this bottleneck, we introduce SyntheticDoc, a massive, high-quality dataset designed to push the boundaries of document unwarping. SyntheticDoc is composed of 1,000,000 high-resolution procedurally generated training samples, alongside extensive validation and test sets. Each sample is paired with rich, pixel-perfect annotations, including UV maps, normal maps, albedo and shading. To ensure physical accuracy and photorealism, the paper geometries are generated via a physics-based simulator and rendered using a path tracer. To demonstrate the benefit of our dataset, we train a simple baseline model on SyntheticDoc and report on its performance in comparison to state-of-the-art methods on both document unwarping and illumination correction tasks. Our dataset is available at https://igl.ethz.ch/projects/SyntheticDoc/ and the code used to generate it at https://github.com/tanguymagne/SyntheticDoc .

</details>


### [176] [Assessing nnU-Net Generalization across Brain Tumor Populations in BraTS-GoAT 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15524)
*Tristan Kirscher,Vivian Metzger,Philippe Meyer,Xavier Coubez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BraTS-GoAT通过3D nnU-Net评估跨异质人群的脑肿瘤分割性能，在1,351个标注案例上训练并在多个验证集上测试，发现源域与目标域之间存在显著的性能差距（Dice从0.9058降至0.8310），失败案例与肿瘤体积较小及增强肿瘤成分分散有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决脑肿瘤分割模型在不同人群间的泛化问题，评估传统nnU-Net在异质人群上的分割性能差距及影响因素。

Method: 使用3D nnU-Net，在1,351个标注案例上采用五折交叉验证训练，每折1,000个epoch，最终预测器对所有折进行平均并应用测试时镜像增强。

Result: 官方验证集上全局DSC为：ET 0.7805, TC 0.8288, WT 0.8854。匹配折0推理时，平均区域Dice从源域出折（OOF）0.9058降至池化验证集0.8310（降幅0.0747）。镜像仅带来小幅单折增益，残差编码器替代方案达0.8282平均Dice。失败案例的参考ET体积明显更小，调整ET和WT体积后，低Dice仍与更多离散ET成分及最大成分中ET占比较小相关。

Conclusion: 传统3D nnU-Net在跨异质人群时存在显著性能下降，增强肿瘤的体积和连通性是影响分割失败的关键因素，需进一步研究提升模型泛化能力。

Abstract: BraTS-GoAT evaluates tumor segmentation across heterogeneous populations. We trained a conventional 3D nnU-Net on 1,351 labeled cases using five-fold cross-validation and 1,000 epochs per fold. The final predictor averaged all folds and applied test-time mirroring. On pooled official validation, global DSC values were 0.7805, 0.8288, and 0.8854 for enhancing tumor (ET), tumor core (TC), and whole tumor (WT). Under matched fold-0 inference, mean regional Dice decreased from 0.9058 on source out-of-fold (OOF) cases to 0.8310 on pooled validation (difference--0.0747). Mirroring gave small single-fold gains but no clear ensemble benefit; a residual-encoder alternative reached 0.8282 mean Dice. In labeled OOF predictions, failure cases had substantially smaller reference ET volumes; after adjustment for ET and WT volume, lower Dice remained associated with more disconnected ET components and a smaller fraction of ET contained in the largest component.

</details>


### [177] [PIVOT: Physics-Grounded Verification for AI-Generated Audio-Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15562)
*Bo Zheng,Kangran Zhao,Xiaoyu Zhang,Weinan Guan,Zhiheng Li,Yize Chen,Haizhou Li,Qingshan Liu,Siwei Lyu,Baoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于物理定律约束的AIGC检测框架PIVOT，通过验证音视频内容是否符合物理定律来区分真实与生成内容，在基准数据集上优于现有大型模型直接检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型进步，AI生成内容(AIGC)越来越逼真，传统的artifact cues检测方法效果下降；但当前生成器仍难以完美复现真实世界事件的物理行为，因此可利用物理定律约束来检测AIGC。

Method: PIVOT通过三个步骤进行检测：1)从视频和音频中估计物理量；2)选择与每个clip相关的物理定律；3)验证物理定律的可测量约束。同时构建了PhysForensics-Bench基准数据集，包含来自9个事件中心场景族和2个音视频生成器的真实与生成音视频配对样本。

Result: 在PhysForensics-Bench上，PIVOT在Real+Seedance上达到70.30%准确率和64.29%F1，在Real+VEO上达到72.16%准确率和65.82%F1；相比之下，Gemini 3.1 Pro直接检查在Real+Seedance上仅获53.96%准确率和60.09%F1，在Real+VEO上获57.22%准确率和63.44%F1。

Conclusion: PIVOT框架通过物理一致性验证方法，在AIGC检测任务上显著优于直接检查方法，在PhysForensics-Bench数据集上实现了约70%的准确率和65%的F1分数，证明了物理约束可作为检测AIGC的有效补充手段。

Abstract: As generative models continue to advance, AI-generated content (AIGC) is becoming increasingly realistic, weakening the artifact cues commonly exploited by existing detectors. Nevertheless, faithfully reproducing the physical behavior of real-world events remains challenging for current generators. We therefore explore detecting AIGC by assessing whether the depicted event satisfies measurable constraints derived from physical laws. We introduce PIVOT, a physics-grounded AIGC detector, instantiated here for audio-video clips, that estimates physical quantities from video and audio, selects physical laws relevant to each clip, and verifies their measurable constraints. Beyond a real/fake decision, PIVOT returns supporting evidence that records the verification outcome, relevant time window, and supporting quantities for each applicable law. Although instantiated and evaluated here on audio-video data, the framework can, in principle, extend to other AIGC modalities whenever the physical quantities required for verification can be estimated reliably. We also introduce PhysForensics-Bench, comprising paired real and generated audio-video clips from nine event-centric scene families and two recent audio-video generators. On PhysForensics-Bench, PIVOT achieves 70.30% accuracy and 64.29% F1 score on Real+Seedance, and 72.16% accuracy and 65.82% F1 on Real+VEO. In comparison, direct inspection with Gemini 3.1 Pro obtains 53.96% accuracy and 60.09% F1 on Real+Seedance, and 57.22% accuracy and 63.44% F1 on Real+Veo. These results demonstrate the practical promise of physical-consistency verification as a structured and inspectable source of evidence that complements artifact-based AIGC detection.

</details>


### [178] [What a gated sensing pipeline never looks at: bandwidth reduction and the misses behind it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15579)
*Raghu Venkat,Tricha Anjali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 空中传感器通过门控感知管道将视频压缩为语义"发现"报告，实现41,977x-155,830x的传输压缩比，但产生压缩的机制与决策机制耦合导致无法独立报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  contested link上无法发送视频，因此转向发送"发现"并报告两者的比率，以实现低带宽传输同时保持语义理解能力。

Method: 结合学习目标检测、图像文本比较与确定性调度、门控、证据积累和传输规则的门控感知管道。包含跟踪器速度测量的校正、在线正常性模型、以及tick级轨迹分析（3,187行）。

Result: 初始测试：38,736比特/211秒，压缩41,977倍，正确识别1/4个 staged事件，无误报。校正后：4次飞行中识别1/7个 staged事件。控制飞行：压缩155,830倍（最大），零报告。轨迹分析显示3个漏检事件分别因：无轨迹、结构位置测试失败(0.129)、边界未达(3.192 vs 3.807)。记录1例已知失败模式：在线正常性模型吸收待判对象。

Conclusion: 研究将空中传感器的视频传输问题转化为"发现"报告问题，通过门控感知管道实现高达155,830倍的数据压缩。证据显示1次检测和6次漏检，作者将其作为案例研究处理。关键发现是产生压缩的机制与决定哪些观察结果到达决策的机制无法分离，这限制了结果的独立可报告性。研究提供了复现协议和生成的成果，但部分支持工件未分发，部分实验未运行。

Abstract: An airborne sensor on a contested link cannot send video, so the appealing move is to send findings instead and report the ratio between the two. We evaluate a gated sensing pipeline that does this, combining learned object detection and image-text comparison with deterministic scheduling, gating, evidence accumulation and transmission rules. On staged footage with the semantic stage live it sends 38,736 bits over 211 s, a reduction of 41,977x, and names 1 of 4 staged events with no false report. That detection has since been superseded: a correction to how the tracker measures speed removed the measurement artefact the normality model had been learning from, and the flight no longer warms. Under the corrected code the pipeline names 1 of 7 staged events across four flights, and we report both. On a control flight where nothing was staged it reports nothing, a reduction of 155,830x: the largest number in the study and the least informative, because a reduction ratio measures the scene. The mechanisms that produce the reduction also decide which observations ever reach a decision, so the two cannot be reported apart. We give a tick-level trace of one flight (3,187 rows) that places each of three missed events at the stage where it stopped progressing: one produced no track, one failed the structural place test at 0.129, and one passed 539 structural ticks but reached only 3.192 against a boundary of 3.807. We also report one instance of a known failure mode, an online normality model absorbing the object it will later judge, measured against the threshold that object then failed. The evidence is one detection and six misses across four staged flights, beside one clean control, and we treat it as a case study. We give the reproduction protocol and generated results, identify which supporting artifacts are not distributed, and state which experiments did not run.

</details>


### [179] [A Unified Vision-Language Model for PSMA PET/CT Report Generation, Visual Question Answering, and Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15603)
*Yang Xing,Jiong Wu,Savas Ozdemir,Yang Zhou,Boxiao Yu,Ying Zhang,Zheren Zhu,Chenyu You,Wei Shao,Yang Lu,Kang Wang,Tinsu Pan,Yang Yang,Kuang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的PSMA PET/CT视觉语言模型，可同时完成报告生成、视觉问答和病变分割三项任务，采用LLaVA架构结合四阶段训练策略，在多项任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PET/CT AI模型仅针对单一任务，缺乏统一的多任务框架；需要一种能够实现结构化、交互式、可解释的PSMA PET/CT分析，并支持 voxel-level 定位的整合模型。

Method: 采用LLaVA风格架构，包含PET/CT视觉编码器、MLP-Mixer投影模块、LoRA调优的大语言模型和3D分割分支；四阶段训练策略：视觉编码器预训练、投影层对齐、VLM微调、多任务调优；语言任务用5,747个PSMA PET/CT数据集，分割任务用AutoPET的PSMA子集。

Result: 报告生成指标优于PET2REP和CT基线；VQA各类问题性能提升；Dice和病变级重叠F1优于SegAnyPET和nnUNet。

Conclusion: 证实了统一多任务框架用于结构化、交互式、可解释PSMA PET/CT分析的可行性，可在单一模型架构内实现voxel-level定位。

Abstract: Accurate PSMA PET/CT interpretation is central to prostate cancer management, yet existing PET/CT AI models typically address isolated tasks. We propose a unified PSMA PET/CT vision-language model for report generation, visual question answering, and lesion segmentation. The framework adopts an LLaVA-style architecture, comprising a PET/CT vision encoder, an MLP-Mixer projection module, a LoRA-tuned large language model, and a 3D segmentation branch. Training followed a four-stage strategy: vision encoder pretraining, projection-layer alignment, VLM fine-tuning, and final multitask tuning. Language tasks used 5,747 PSMA PET/CT datasets with paired reports, while segmentation used the PSMA subset of AutoPET. The model outperformed PET2REP and a CT-based baseline across standard report-generation metrics, improved performance across VQA question types, and achieved higher Dice and lesion-level overlap F1 than SegAnyPET and nnUNet. These results support the feasibility of a unified framework for structured, interactive, interpretable PSMA PET/CT analysis with voxel-level grounding within a single multitask model architecture.

</details>


### [180] [VideoScout: Learning Agentic Active Exploration with Adaptive Reasoning Pacing for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15606)
*Weixin Xu,Zhenyu Yang,Bing Wang,Shengsheng Qian,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoScout提出了一种用于长视频理解的多智能体推理方法，将长视频理解形式化为顺序证据获取(SEA)问题。该方法通过自适应推理节奏动态控制观看速度，在有限视觉上下文窗口内高效遍历长视频。训练采用两阶段管道：冷启动监督微调+DAPO强化学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在长视频理解方面受限于有限的视觉上下文窗口。现有方法（均匀帧采样或粗到细的代理缩放）难以在足够长的视频中定位稀疏的决定性证据。

Method: 1)提出SEA范式：智能体沿时间轴逐段阅读视频，每轮决定观看速度、保留证据、是否回溯不确定片段、何时停止回答；2)构建VideoScout-66K数据集（10K验证轨迹的66K高质量探索回合）；3)两阶段训练：冷启动监督微调+DAPO算法（结合答案准确性、输出格式合规性、时间对齐IoU的复合奖励）

Result: 7B模型在长视频理解和推理基准测试中取得与现有训练7B代理模型相比的强性能

Conclusion: SEA范式和VideoScout方法为长视频理解提供了新思路，通过自适应观看节奏在有限上下文窗口内高效遍历长视频内容

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress on short video understanding yet remain limited on long videos due to the limited visual context window. Prevailing approaches rely on uniform frame sampling or recent coarse-to-fine agentic zooming, both of which struggle to localize sparse, decisive evidence in sufficiently long videos. We formulate long video understanding as a \textbf{{Sequential Evidence Acquisition (SEA)}} problem, in which an agent reads the video turn by turn along the temporal axis, deciding at each turn how fast to watch, what evidence to retain, when to revisit uncertain segments, and when to stop and answer. Inspired by this view, we propose \textbf{{VideoScout}}, a multi-turn reasoning agent that instantiates the SEA paradigm through adaptive reasoning pacing. Specifically, by dynamically controlling the viewing pace, VideoScout enables efficient traversal of long videos within a bounded visual context window, allowing the agent to access more video content while balancing content analysis depth with reading efficiency. To train VideoScout, we construct VideoScout-66K, a set of over 66K high-quality exploration turns from 10K answer-verified trajectories, and adopt a two-stage pipeline: cold-start supervised fine-tuning teaches the agent per-turn output format, while the Decoupled Clip and Dynamic sAmpling Policy Optimization (DAPO) algorithm performs trajectory-level reinforcement learning with a composite reward that jointly considers answer accuracy, output format compliance, and the temporal alignment between the agent's viewing progress and the teacher's answer timing measured by intersection-over-union (IoU). Extensive experiments on long video understanding and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our 7B model achieves strong performance compared with existing trained 7B agentic models.

</details>


### [181] [ModaLens: Measuring Image Sensitivity in Report-Conditioned Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15635)
*Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Maximin Lange,Quang Bui,Anqi Peter Li,Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Rafi Al Attrach,Kushul Reddy Palakala,Sahil Kapadia,Zakaria Laouabdia Sellami,Xinyue Zhang,Ashley Zhang,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ModaLens提出了一种配对图像替换审计方法，用于检测视觉语言模型在生成放射学报告时是否真正使用图像。通过在MIMIC-CXR数据集上测试MedGemma-27B模型，发现报告可用性显著降低图像替换敏感性（从20.94%降至4.26%），表明模型在有报告时减少了对图像的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告已能回答临床问题，难以判断视觉语言模型是否真正使用了图像。现有评估方法无法有效区分模型是依赖图像还是仅依赖文本报告。

Method: 配对图像替换审计（paired image-swap audit）：在3,199个MIMIC-CXR病例（293名患者）上，将每个病例的图像替换为另一研究（通常同一患者）的图像，同时保持问题和报告不变，测量模型答案变化率。

Result: MedGemma-27B在显式答案指令下，有报告时答案变化4.26%，无报告时变化20.94%（配对增加16.7点，患者聚类95%CI 15.6-17.7）。原始提示词给出4.70%对比17.07%。该方向在两个其他模型系列中复现。

Conclusion: 报告可用性降低了模型对图像替换的敏感性，表明在有报告的情况下，模型可能减少图像使用。标签来源于报告限制了关于视觉正确性的结论。该方法可推广用于评估其他视觉语言模型是否真正利用图像。

Abstract: A radiology report can already answer a clinical question, so it is hard to tell whether a vision-language model also uses the image. ModaLens, a paired image-swap audit, measures how report availability changes image sensitivity: MedGemma-27B on 3,199 paired MIMIC-CXR cases from 293 patients, all 14 questions per case (13 finding-specific and one composite), each image replaced by one from another study, usually of the same patient, with question and report fixed. Under an explicit answer instruction, the model's generated answer changes on 4.26 percent of trials with the report and 20.94 percent without it, a paired increase of 16.7 points (patient-clustered 95 percent CI 15.6 to 17.7), so report availability reduces image-swap sensitivity under this protocol; the original prompt with a lowercase first-token readout gives 4.70 percent against 17.07 percent, and substitutions also move continuous answer scores where the binary prediction does not change. The labels are derived from reports, which limits conclusions about visual correctness; the direction replicates in two further model lineages. Code, the exact prompts and a run record for every number are at https://github.com/criticaldata/MODALENS.

</details>


### [182] [SAM3D-Part: Interactive Part Selection and Generation from 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15639)
*Jiahao Chang,Dong Du,Wanhu Sun,Yujian Zheng,Chuanyu Pan,Bowen Zhao,Chongjie Ye,Yuanming Hu,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM3D-Part是一个提示驱动的选择性部件生成框架，可根据源3D网格和部件提示生成完整的可重用部件网格，解决现有方法需要生成全部部件或只能输出部分表面的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代3D资产生成中需要频繁编辑、复用、动画化或制造各个组件，但现有方法要么生成全部部件而不顾用户意图，要么分割方法只能输出部分表面而非完整可重用网格，且图像条件部件生成器难以保留隐藏几何和准确放置。

Method: 1）将源几何编码为紧凑网格特征并与渲染图像、选择性掩码和点图观察通过像素级通道融合对齐；2）融合表示条件化前馈生成模型生成查询部件的完整网格；3）预测密集体素对应并从分布式空间证据估计部件变换；4）多部件查询时将先前生成的部件存储在部件缓存中作为上下文约束重用。

Result: 显著提高源对齐效果，降低条件化成本，实现一致的选择性部件生成，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: SAM3D-Part能够实现高质量的选择性部件生成，为3D资产生成提供了更灵活高效的工作流程。

Abstract: Part-level control is essential for modern 3D asset creation, where objects are frequently edited, reused, animated, or fabricated through their individual components. In many such workflows, users need only several specific components rather than a complete object decomposition. However, existing 3D generation methods produce all parts regardless of user intent, while promptable 3D segmentation methods typically output partial surfaces instead of reusable complete meshes. In addition, image-conditioned part generators further struggle to preserve hidden geometry and accurate placement without directly conditioning on the source mesh. To address these problems, we present SAM3D-Part, a prompt-driven framework for selective part generation from input 3D object meshes. Given a source mesh and a part prompt, SAM3D-Part first encodes the source geometry into compact mesh features and aligns them with the rendered image, selective mask, and point-map observations via pixel-wise channel fusion. The fused representation conditions a feed-forward generative model to produce only the queried component as a completed mesh. To place the generated part back into the source coordinate frame, SAM3D-Part predicts dense per-voxel correspondences and estimates the part transformation from distributed spatial evidence rather than a single global pose code. For sequential multi-part queries, previously generated parts are stored in a part cache and reused as contextual constraints, reducing conflicts among independently requested components. Extensive experiments and ablations demonstrate that SAM3D-Part can significantly improve source alignment, reduce conditioning cost, and enable consistent selective part generation, achieving state-of-the-art. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/Jiahao620/sam3d-part.

</details>


### [183] [Human-Grounded Calibration for Long-Text Image-Text Congruence in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15640)
*Alessandro Gambetti,Qiwei Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Congruency Score (CS)，一种轻量级校准层，用于将图像-文本相似度证据映射为有界分数，以解决长文本图像-文本一致性评分问题。研究发现后投影质心距离的减少并不能统一提升检索性能，且直接后验校准与人类判断的关联性更高，而基于投影的配置可减少阈值相关的斜率/截距畸变，但会牺牲检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言系统中详细文本描述与视觉内容的一致性评分越来越重要，但双编码器模型的原始相似度分数难以解释为校准后的一致性度量，尤其在图像和文本嵌入之间的模态差距情况下。

Method: 提出 Congruency Score (CS)，一种轻量级校准层，将图像-文本相似度证据映射为有界分数。使用 DOCCI 和 Urban1k 数据集评估四个冻结的视觉语言主干网络。

Result: 后投影质心距离的减少并不能统一提升图像-文本检索性能。人类在 DOCCI 上的评估揭示了权衡：直接后验校准保持与人类判断的高关联性，而选定的基于投影的配置可减少阈值相关的斜率和截距畸变，但以检索性能和关联强度为代价。

Conclusion: 长文本图像-文本一致性评分应作为校准的分数估计问题来对待，检索性能、人类关联和阈值校准必须作为不同目标进行评估。CS 提供了一种轻量级方式来暴露和操作这种分离。

Abstract: Long-text image--text congruence scoring is increasingly important for vision-language systems that must evaluate whether detailed textual descriptions match visual content. However, raw similarity scores from dual-encoder models are difficult to interpret as calibrated congruence measures, especially under the modality gap between image and text embeddings. This paper proposes Congruency Score (CS), a lightweight calibration layer that maps image--text similarity evidence into a bounded score. Using DOCCI and Urban1k, we evaluate four frozen vision-language backbones and show that observed reductions in post-projection centroid distance do not uniformly improve image--text retrieval performance. Human-grounded evaluations on DOCCI further reveal a trade-off: direct post-hoc calibration preserves high association with human judgments, whereas selected projection-based configurations can reduce threshold-relevant slope and intercept distortions at the cost of retrieval performance and association strength. These results establish long-text image--text congruence scoring as a calibrated score-estimation problem, where retrieval performance, human association, and threshold calibration must be evaluated as distinct objectives. CS provides a lightweight way to expose and operationalize this separation.

</details>


### [184] [MedDiME: Efficient Latent Diffusion with Adaptive Masking for Medical Counterfactual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15647)
*Yan Zeng,Changlu Guo,Anders Nymark Christensen,Morten Rieger Hannemose,Anders Bjorholm Dahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedDiME是一个潜在的分类器引导扩散框架，用于医学反事实生成，通过梯度驱动的自适应掩码机制实现空间精确的医学图像修改，相比之前的分类器引导扩散基线方法，推理速度提升40倍，峰值GPU内存降低13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的医学反事实生成方法存在计算成本高、内存消耗大的问题，难以应用于高分辨率场景；同时现有掩码策略与像素空间紧密耦合，与潜在空间扩散编辑不兼容。

Method: 提出MedDiME框架，采用潜在空间分类器引导扩散，在潜在空间中进行反事实生成以降低计算和内存开销；引入潜在兼容的梯度驱动自适应掩码机制，实现空间精确的医学反事实生成。

Result: 实验表明MedDiME实现了高质量的反事实生成，相比现有分类器引导扩散基线方法，推理速度提升高达40倍，峰值GPU内存降低13倍。

Conclusion: MedDiME在保持生成质量的同时显著提升了效率，为医学图像解释性分析提供了一种更实用的方法。

Abstract: Medical counterfactual generation modifies images to change model predictions for interpretability. However, existing diffusion-based approaches are often prohibitively slow and memory-intensive, making them difficult to apply in high-resolution settings. Moreover, existing masking strategies are tightly coupled with pixel-space representations, making them incompatible with latent-space diffusion editing. To address these challenges, we propose MedDiME, a latent-space classifier-guided diffusion framework that reduces computational and memory overhead while introducing a latent-compatible, gradient-driven adaptive masking mechanism for spatially precise medical counterfactual generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MedDiME achieves high-quality counterfactual generation with significant efficiency gains compared to prior classifier-guided diffusion baselines, achieving up to 40 times faster inference and 13 times lower peak GPU memory usage.

</details>


### [185] [From Model Patterns to Abstract Semantics in Compositional Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15649)
*Weize Li,Zhicheng Zhao,Fei Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Compositional Zero Shot Learning aims to recognize unseen compositions by recombining learned primitives. Recent methods rely on vision language models and attempt to explicitly model contextual variations of primitives through multiple representations. However, such approaches are limited by fixed variant capacity and competition between abstract and concrete semantics. In this work, we present a new perspective that views primitive variations as the context-driven activation of concrete visual cues rather than independent entities. Based on it, we propose CLEAR, a CLoze-style rEAsoning-based Re-ranking framework inspired by human perceptual processes. CLEAR extracts conditional variants from the primitive candidate set in a coarse-to-fine manner, performs cloze-style reasoning to infer high-level semantics, and re-ranks predictions to correct biases toward salient concrete primitives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CLEAR consistently improves the Base Model and outperforms state-of-the-art methods on the challenging C-GQA and MIT-States datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/buptLwz/CLEAR.

</details>


### [186] [Circuit-MLLM: Topological Logic-Guided Latent-Space Visual Reasoning for Circuit Schematic Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15668)
*Jinyuan Deng,Yuqi Jiang,Wenjing Huang,Xin Li,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Circuit-MLLM，一个用于电路原理图分析的多模态大语言模型框架，通过电路知识挖掘机制和拓扑引导排序策略实现电路拓扑结构的细粒度解析，在电路分析任务中平均得分比GPT-5.1高25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在通用任务上表现优异，但电路原理图因其密集的组件布局和独特的拓扑逻辑对MLLMs构成挑战，需要细粒度的结构解析来提取电气语义。

Method: 1)提出电路知识挖掘机制：将模型的潜在表征与来自多粒度电路视觉专家的结构化特征深度对齐，使模型能够有效内化拓扑语义；2)设计拓扑引导排序策略：在潜在空间中沿电路拓扑逻辑进行逐步推理，摆脱刚性光栅扫描顺序的束缚。

Result: 在多样化的电路分析任务中，Circuit-MLLM始终优于强基线模型，平均得分比GPT-5.1高25%。

Conclusion: Circuit-MLLM框架在电路原理图拓扑分析方面表现出色，其电路知识挖掘机制和拓扑引导排序策略能有效提升模型对电路拓扑结构的理解和推理能力。

Abstract: Through pre-training on extensive text and image datasets, current multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on general tasks. However, circuit schematics present a unique challenge for MLLMs due to their dense component layouts and distinct topological logic, demanding fine-grained structural parsing to extract the electrical semantics. To address this, we propose Circuit-MLLM, a multimodal reasoning framework that reformulates circuit topology analysis as a process of device localization, path tracing, and sequential reasoning within the latent space. We introduce a circuit knowledge mining mechanism that deeply aligns the model's latent representations with structurally rich features derived from multi-granularity circuit vision experts, enabling the model to effectively internalize topological semantics. Building upon these internalized semantics, we devise a topology-guided sequencing strategy that decouples reasoning from the rigid raster-scan order, enforcing stepwise inference along the circuit's topological logic in latent space. Across diverse circuit analysis tasks, Circuit-MLLM consistently outperforms strong baselines, notably achieving a 25% higher average score than GPT-5.1, which demonstrates the effectiveness of our framework in circuit schematic topology analysis. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/IC-Yuan/Circuit-MLLM.

</details>


### [187] [Benchmarking Intra-Patient 3D Deformable Multimodal Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15669)
*Matteo Barbieri,Giammarco La Barbera,Juan Pablo De La Plata,Sabine Sarnacki,Isabelle Bloch,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对 intra-patient 3D 多模态 deformable registration 方法进行了综合 benchmark 评估，涵盖三个数据集、多种经典优化方法和现代 learning-based 方法。核心发现是：几何指标（DSC、HD95）与图像相似度指标（MIND-SSC）之间存在一致的分歧，且目前没有方法能在不同解剖结构和模态下实现 robust performance。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像配准是许多临床工作流程的关键组件，但由于对应解剖结构在不同模态间表现出显著不同的图像强度，至今仍具有挑战性。之前缺乏对不同解剖区域和难度级别的综合 benchmark 评估。

Method: 评估了经典优化方法（optimization-based approaches）和现代 learning-based 方法（包括深度学习和基础模型），使用三个数据集和三类指标：DSC（Dice similarity coefficient）、HD95（95th-percentile Hausdorff distance）、MIND-SSC（modality-independent structural similarity）。

Result: Learning-based 方法在大规模 synthetic benchmarks 上表现优越，但在 real pelvic registration 上仅观察到有限改进。几何指标（DSC、HD95）与图像相似度指标（MIND-SSC）之间存在一致的分歧——改善的重叠不一定意味着更好的全局多模态对应。Anatomy-guided 方法达到最高重叠分数，但在分割区域外表现退化，揭示了 label-driven alignment 与 global structural coherence 之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 目前没有方法能在不同解剖结构和模态下实现 robust performance。Intra-patient 3D multimodal registration 需要多标准评估（包括基于 deformation 的指标），仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Multimodal image registration is a key component of many clinical workflows, yet it remains challenging because corresponding anatomical structures often exhibit substantially different image intensities across modalities. In this work, we present a comprehensive benchmark of intra-patient 3D multimodal deformable registration methods across three datasets covering different anatomical regions and difficulty levels, including both synthetic deformation recovery and real clinical scenarios. We evaluate classical optimization-based approaches and modern learning-based methods, including recent deep learning and foundation models, using complementary metrics: Average Dice similarity coefficient (DSC), average 95th-percentile Hausdorff distance (HD95), and a modality-independent structural similarity measure based on the MIND self-similarity context (MIND-SSC). Results show high variability across datasets, with learning-based methods demonstrating superior performance on large synthetic benchmarks, while only limited improvements are observed in real pelvic registration. A key finding of this study is the consistent disagreement between geometric metrics (DSC, HD95) and image-based similarity metrics (MIND-SSC), highlighting that improved overlap does not necessarily imply better global multimodal correspondence. Furthermore, anatomy-guided approaches achieve the highest overlap scores but exhibit degraded performance outside of segmented regions, revealing a trade-off between label-driven alignment and global structural coherence. Overall, our results indicate that no current method achieves robust performance across anatomies and modalities. We demonstrate that intra-patient 3D multimodal registration requires multi-criteria evaluation, including deformation-based metrics, and remains an open problem.

</details>


### [188] [Don't Send What You Don't Need: Question-Guided Token Pruning as a Privacy Defense for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15671)
*Md Khalid Syfullah,Alvi Ataur Khalil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QPriv-VL是一个问题引导的、隐私感知的视觉token剪枝框架，用于联邦学习、分割学习和U形分割学习中的视觉问答任务。它通过动态阈值预测器(DTP)在单次前向传播中同时估计样本特定的剪枝比例和token级保留掩码，结合问题相关性和隐私敏感性信号来剪枝视觉token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐私敏感和带宽受限的设置中，视觉问答任务使用视觉语言模型时，现有的联邦学习方法虽然保留原始数据本地化，但传输所有视觉token仍然成本高昂且可能暴露隐私信息。需要一种方法在传输前剪枝视觉token，同时平衡任务效用和隐私保护。

Method: 提出QPriv-VL框架，核心组件是轻量级动态阈值预测器(DTP)。DTP在一个前向传播中联合估计样本特定的剪枝比例和token级保留掩码。它结合了两种信号：1)问题相关性，通过视觉patch和池化问题嵌入之间的跨模态相似度计算；2)敏感性信号，来自冻结的DINOv2特征。无需敏感性标签即可抑制敏感区域同时保留对回答问题有用的patch。

Result: 在GQA、OK-VQA、VQAv2、SLAKE、VQA-RAD和PathVQA数据集上评估，针对四种隐私攻击家族(FSHA、FORA、iDLG和属性推理成员推断攻击)进行测试。DTP达到或优于固定比例剪枝，且使用更少的传输token。在VQA-RAD上，将成员推断攻击成功率从0.99降低到0.76-0.79，降低FSHA和FORA重建PSNR，保留竞争性VQA准确率同时仅使用约40%的原始视觉token预算。敏感性排除比为1.20±0.18，表明优先移除隐私敏感patch。可解释性分析显示保留适应问题语义而非通用视觉显著性。

Conclusion: QPriv-VL成功实现了视觉token的高效剪枝，在保护隐私的同时保持VQA任务性能。该框架适用于FL、SL和USL等多种分布式学习场景，无需敏感性标签即可有效识别和移除敏感视觉信息，为隐私保护的视觉问答系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is increasingly used in privacy-sensitive and bandwidth-constrained settings. Federated Learning (FL), Split Learning (SL), and U-Shaped Split Learning (USL) keep raw data local, but transmitting all visual tokens across a model partition remains costly and can expose private information. We propose QPriv-VL, a question-guided, privacy-aware token-pruning framework for FL, SL, and USL that prunes visual tokens before transmission based on task utility and privacy sensitivity. Its core component is a lightweight Dynamic Threshold Predictor (DTP) that jointly estimates a sample-specific pruning ratio and a token-level retention mask in one forward pass. DTP combines question relevance, computed from cross-modal similarity between visual patches and the pooled question embedding, with a sensitivity signal derived from frozen DINOv2 features. This allows the model to suppress potentially sensitive regions while preserving patches useful for answering the question, without requiring sensitivity labels. We evaluate QPriv-VL on GQA, OK-VQA, VQAv2, SLAKE, VQA-RAD, and PathVQA against four privacy attack families: FSHA, FORA, iDLG, and attribute-inference membership inference attacks. DTP matches or outperforms fixed-ratio pruning while using substantially fewer transmitted tokens. On VQA-RAD, it reduces membership-inference attack success from 0.99 to 0.76-0.79, lowers FSHA and FORA reconstruction PSNR relative to fixed-ratio pruning, and preserves competitive VQA accuracy using about 40% of the original visual-token budget. A sensitivity exclusion ratio of 1.20 +/- 0.18 indicates preferential removal of privacy-sensitive patches, while explainability analysis shows that retention adapts to question semantics rather than generic visual saliency.

</details>


### [189] [MambaMPD: A Mamba-Driven Segmentation Framework for Marine Pollution Detection from Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15676)
*Shuaiyu Chen,Wei Han,Peng Ren,Chunbo Luo,Zeyu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MambaMPD框架，基于Vision Mamba模型进行海洋污染检测，通过频率感知增强(FAA)和多尺度边缘引导注意力(EGA)两个结构先验解决海洋污染检测中低信噪比、污染模式碎片化和边界模糊的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋污染检测(MPD)面临低信噪比、污染模式碎片化、污染物与周围海域视觉相似导致的边界模糊等难题，传统Vision Mamba模型在MPD领域的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出MambaMPD框架，包含两个核心模块：1) FAA频率感知增强：将小波变换集成到编码器中，将特征分解为多尺度频率子带，捕获低频上下文语义和高频结构细节；2) EGA边缘引导注意力：自适应融合拉普拉斯衍生的边界线索与深度语义表示，在解码前精炼编码器特征。采用带挤压激励注意力和深度监督的U-Net风格解码器逐步恢复和精炼跨尺度的语义和空间信息。

Result: 在两个基准MPD数据集上的广泛实验表明，MambaMPD实现更高的mIoU，同时比基础模型方法需要更少计算量。在MADOS上比OSDMamba提升F1 3.6%；在M4D上比TransOilSeg提升Oil Spill IoU 6.82%。

Conclusion: FAA和EGA模块共同提高了对微弱污染信号的敏感性，同时保留了精细的边界结构，表明该方法在海洋污染检测任务中具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Accurate marine pollution detection (MPD) is essential for protecting coastal ecosystems and marine biodiversity. Vision Mamba models have shown promise in remote-sensing semantic segmentation by efficiently capturing long-range dependencies and global context, yet their potential for MPD remains underexplored. MPD is particularly challenging because of low signal-to-noise ratios, fragmented pollution patterns, and indistinct boundaries caused by the visual similarity between pollutants and the surrounding sea. To address these issues, we propose MambaMPD, an enhanced Mamba-based framework incorporating two complementary structural priors: Frequency-Aware Augmentation (FAA) and multi-scale Edge-Guided Attention (EGA). FAA integrates wavelet transforms into the encoder to decompose features into multi-scale frequency subbands, enabling the model to capture low-frequency contextual semantics and high-frequency structural details needed to identify small, low-contrast, and irregular pollution patterns. EGA adaptively fuses hierarchical, Laplacian-derived boundary cues with deep semantic representations, refining encoder features before decoding to sharpen boundaries and reduce ambiguity in visually confusing, spatially fragmented scenes. Together, these modules improve sensitivity to subtle pollution signals while preserving fine boundary structures. A U-Net-style decoder with squeeze-and-excitation attention and deep supervision progressively restores and refines semantic and spatial information across scales. Extensive experiments on two benchmark MPD datasets show that MambaMPD achieves higher mIoU than competing methods while requiring substantially less computation than foundation-model-based approaches. On MADOS, it improves F1 by 3.6% over OSDMamba; on M4D, it raises Oil Spill IoU by 6.82% over TransOilSeg.

</details>


### [190] [V-ICAL Bench: Evaluating Video In-Context Learning for Multimodal Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15683)
*Ziqian Fan,Shibo Xu,Junjie Li,Xiangyu Zhao,Shengyuan Ding,Yifan Yang,Zhenjie Yang,Haodong Duan,Yue Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: V-ICAL是一个用于评估多模态智能体视频上下文学习能力的新基准，包含342个交互任务和37个环境。现有最先进的模型表现远低于人类基线，暴露了多模态智能体ICL能力的重大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态上下文学习(ICL)尤其是视频演示在交互环境中的应用仍基本未被探索。视频演示对多模态智能体提出了独特挑战：需要将上下文演示转化为可执行策略，在新视觉状态中建立策略基础，并根据环境反馈迭代优化动作。

Method: 构建包含342个交互任务的基准，涵盖37个环境，使用人类策划的演示视频作为任务特定行为示例，通过从目标初始化的持续交互来评估智能体。基准连接了上下文知识归纳与智能体核心能力，包括状态基础、时间记忆、规划和动态环境适应。

Result: 最佳模型Seed-2.1-Pro仅获54.4分(满分100)，Gemini-3.1-Pro、GPT-5.6等领先模型均未超过50分，远低于人类基线83.6分。对照实验表明当前智能体难以将视频示例可靠转化为有效策略，未能产生一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: V-ICAL揭示了多模态智能体ICL能力的关键差距，迫切需要未来研究关注。这一发现为多模态学习和智能体领域的重要研究方向。

Abstract: While In-Context Learning (ICL) enables models to adapt from exemplars without parameter updates, multimodal ICL remains largely underexplored, particularly regarding video demonstrations in interactive environments. For multimodal agents, learning from videos presents unique challenges: they must translate in-context demonstrations into executable policies, ground these policies in novel visual states, and iteratively refine actions based on environmental feedback. We introduce V-ICAL, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate video-based ICL for multimodal agents. Comprising 342 interactive tasks across 37 environments, V-ICAL utilizes human-curated demonstration videos as task-specific behavioral exemplars, evaluating agents through sustained interaction from a target initialization. The benchmark seamlessly connects in-context knowledge induction with core agentic capabilities, including state grounding, temporal memory, planning, and adaptation in dynamic environments. Extensive evaluations across 19 state-of-the-art multimodal agents reveal significant limitations: the best-performing model, Seed-2.1-Pro, achieves a score of only 54.4/100, while other leading models (e.g., Gemini-3.1-Pro, GPT-5.6) fail to surpass 50, far below the human baseline of 83.6. Controlled comparisons further demonstrate that current agents struggle to reliably translate video exemplars into effective policies, failing to yield consistent performance gains. Ultimately, V-ICAL exposes a critical gap in the ICL capabilities of multimodal agents, underscoring an urgent need for future research.

</details>


### [191] [SURE-Map: Self-Correcting Streaming Geometric Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15795)
*Mingkai Liu,Hao Zhao,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SURE-Map是一个基于流式几何基础模型的自纠正重建框架，通过显式建模跨视图几何不确定性和多时间尺度自纠正来解决流式重建中的误差累积问题，在长距离基准测试中实现了新的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式几何基础模型虽然可以替代SLAM系统，但每个预测仅基于有限上下文，容易受到动态物体和弱纹理影响，小的局部误差会累积成严重的几何畸变和长期尺度漂移，需要模型不仅具有预测能力，还要具有自纠正能力。

Method: 提出两个互补原则：一是显式建模跨视图几何不确定性，直接衡量联合预测的位姿和深度是否产生几何一致的跨视图像素对应；二是多时间尺度自纠正，快节奏连续帧推理保持流式效率，稀疏关键帧窗口推理提供更长程几何证据定期重新校准轨迹尺度。

Result: 在长距离基准测试中建立新的最先进在线前馈重建性能：KITTI上ATE-RMSE从24.00降至17.24米，Oxford Spires从5.11降至4.74米，VBR从31.37降至28.58米；结合闭环 refinement后进一步改善至15.17、4.63和22.12米。

Conclusion: SURE-Map通过自纠正机制有效解决了流式几何基础模型的误差累积问题，跨视图几何不确定性和多时间尺度自纠正是两个关键创新，为在线重建提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Streaming geometric foundation models are emerging as a compelling alternative to SLAM systems. Yet this streaming nature introduces a fundamental issue: each prediction is made from limited context, which is vulnerable to dynamic objects and weak textures. Small local errors accumulate into severe geometric distortion and long-horizon scale drift. We argue that reliable streaming reconstruction requires geometric foundation models to be not only predictive, but also self-correcting. We introduce SURE-Map, a self-correcting framework built upon two complementary principles. First, we explicitly model cross-view geometric uncertainty. Unlike conventional depth or point confidence, which primarily reflects the reliability of individual-view prediction, our uncertainty directly measures whether the jointly predicted pose and depth induce geometrically consistent cross-view pixel correspondences. Second, because local correction alone cannot eliminate slowly accumulating scale errors, we introduce multi-timescale self-correction: fast consecutive-frame inference preserves streaming efficiency, while sparse keyframe-window inference provides longer-range geometric evidence to periodically recalibrate the scale of recent trajectories. SURE-Map establishes new state-of-the-art performance for online feed-forward reconstruction across long-horizon benchmarks, reducing ATE-RMSE from 24.00 to 17.24 m on KITTI, 5.11 to 4.74 m on Oxford Spires, and 31.37 to 28.58 m on VBR, with further improvements to 15.17, 4.63, and 22.12 m when incorporating loop-closure refinement. Project page: https://mingkai-liu.github.io/projects/sure-map/.

</details>


### [192] [VC-Attention: Value Smoothing and Softmax Casting for Low-bit Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15810)
*Xingyang Li,Dongyun Zou,Shining Zhang,Jiacheng Chen,Haocheng Xi,Lvmin Zhang,Jun-Yan Zhu,Song Han,Zhekai Zhang,Yujun Lin,Muyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VC-Attention是一种训练无关的低比特注意力框架，通过Value平滑(V-Smooth)和融合概率转换(ExpCast-FP8)解决Diffusion Transformer部署中outliers导致的精度问题和softmax导致的加速瓶颈，在多款GPU上实现1.46-3.6x的注意力kernel加速和1.13-1.70x的端到端生成加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers用于视频生成时，长时空序列导致注意力成为主要部署成本，低比特部署核必须同时具备高精度和高速性。现有方法受两方面限制：1) 精度受限由于outliers——块的量化尺度由最大元素设定，导致典型元素只能表示在狭窄范围内；2) 速度受限由于softmax——低比特Tensor Core仅加速两次矩阵乘法，其间的高精度指数运算成为数据中心GPU上最长的流水线阶段。

Method: 提出两项核心技术：1) V-Smooth通过轻量级在线聚类重排value tokens，使同一硬件块内的tokens能够良好量化，仅对减去块均值后的残差进行量化，并从在线softmax已维护的行和恢复均值；2) ExpCast-FP8将log域分数直接映射到E4M3概率码，使用一次融合乘加操作，消除FP32指数运算和格式转换。针对B200、B300、H200、RTX PRO 6000和RTX 5090实现了VC-Attention。

Result: 在Wan2.2、LongCat-Video、HunyuanVideo-1.5和MiniMax-H3上，VC-Attention相比低比特基线提升保真度；在数据中心Blackwell和Hopper上kernel加速1.46-1.59x，在工作站显卡上加速2.3-3.6x；端到端生成加速方面，数据中心1.13-1.19x，工作站1.36-1.70x。

Conclusion: VC-Attention成功实现了训练无关的低比特注意力加速，在数据中心GPU和工作站显卡上分别实现1.46-1.59x和2.3-3.6x的kernel加速，以及端到端1.13-1.70x的生成加速，为Diffusion Transformer的大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers deliver state-of-the-art video generation, but their long spatiotemporal sequences make attention the dominant deployment cost, and a deployable low-bit kernel must be accurate and fast. Accuracy is limited by outliers: a block's quantization scale is set by its largest entries, leaving typical entries confined to a narrow range of representable values. Prior work smooths queries and keys, but value outliers follow no fixed channel or spatiotemporal structure and remain the dominant source of output error. Speed is limited by softmax: low-bit Tensor Cores accelerate only the two matrix multiplications, so the high-precision exponential between them becomes the longest pipeline stage on datacenter GPUs. We propose VC-Attention, a training-free low-bit attention framework that addresses both by pairing Value smoothing with a fused probability Cast. V-Smooth reorders value tokens by lightweight online clustering, so the tokens in a hardware block quantize well together. It quantizes only the residual after subtracting the block mean, and restores that mean from the row sum the online softmax already maintains. ExpCast-FP8 maps log-domain scores directly to E4M3 probability codes with one fused multiply-add, eliminating the FP32 exponential and the format conversion. We implement VC-Attention for B200, B300, H200, RTX PRO 6000, and RTX 5090. Across Wan2.2, LongCat-Video, HunyuanVideo-1.5, and MiniMax-H3, VC-Attention improves fidelity over low-bit baselines, speeds up the attention kernel over BF16 FlashAttention-4 by 1.46-1.59x on datacenter Blackwell and Hopper and by 2.3-3.6x on workstation cards, and generates a clip 1.13-1.19x and 1.36-1.70x faster end to end.

</details>


### [193] [Integrating Multi-view Multi-light Surface Reconstruction into Cultural Heritage Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15833)
*Baptiste Brument,Robin Bruneau,Benjamin Coupry,Vincent Demoulin,Jean Mélou,Antoine Laurent,Fabien Castan,Jean-Denis Durou,Lilian Calvet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将多视角、多光照三维表面重建的先进计算机视觉方法集成到开源摄影测量软件Meshroom中，为文化遗产记录提供更易用的工作流程。该系统整合了完整的光度立体生态系统（校准、自校准和通用版本）、自动对象遮罩和多视角法线-反射率融合功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统摄影测量软件未能充分利用多视角、多光照数据的潜力来改进细尺度表面重建。文化遗产领域已使用RTI dome等受控光照采集设备，但难以与现有计算机视觉方法结合。需要在现有采集实践与最新计算机视觉方法之间建立连接，形成可操作的文化遗产工作流程。

Method: 将先进的计算机视觉组件集成到开源摄影测量框架Meshroom中，包括：完整的光度立体生态系统（校准、自校准和通用三种模式）、自动对象遮罩功能、以及多视角法线和反射率融合技术。这些组件被整合成一个易于使用的遗产导向工作流程，作为计算机视觉研究与实际文化遗产应用之间的中间软件层。

Result: 创建了一个实用的文化遗产导向工作流程，使先进的细尺度表面重建技术易于使用和评估。该系统连接了现有的RTI dome等采集设备与最新的计算机视觉方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的系统在计算机视觉研究和实际文化遗产应用之间提供了中间软件层，使最新技术更易于遗产专业人士使用和评估，推动了文化遗产保护中三维重建技术的发展。

Abstract: Cultural heritage documentation increasingly relies on image-based 3D surface reconstruction, with photogrammetry software making such workflows accessible to archaeologists, conservators, and heritage technicians. These tools have been successful for conventional multi-view acquisition, but they do not routinely exploit richer multi-view, multi-light data, despite its potential for improving fine-scale surface reconstruction. This limitation is particularly relevant in heritage contexts, where controlled-light acquisition devices such as RTI domes are already used to capture illumination-varying image sets. The challenge is therefore to connect these existing acquisition practices with recent computer vision methods in a form that can be used within operational heritage workflows. In this work, we address this need by integrating state-of-the-art components from computer vision for multi-view, multi-light surface reconstruction into Meshroom, an open-source photogrammetry framework. Rather than proposing a new reconstruction algorithm, our contribution is to assemble and expose existing advanced methods, namely a complete photometric stereo ecosystem (calibrated, self-calibrated and universal), automatic object masking, and multi-view normal-and-reflectance integration, within a usable heritage-oriented workflow. The proposed system thus provides an intermediate software layer between computer vision research code and practical cultural heritage applications, making recent techniques easier to use and evaluate.

</details>


### [194] [TRACE: Two-Stage Detector-Response Estimation With Angular Cosine Expansion for Ring Artifact Correction in Photon-Counting CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15834)
*Jigang Duan,Heran Wang,Ligen Shi,Zheng Sun,Ping Yang,Xing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRACE方法，一种用于估计和校正光子计数探测器CT中响应相关误差的两阶段无监督正弦图分解方法。该方法将条纹表示为固定偏置加上低阶离散余弦变换分量，使用可学习的分析-综合架构来估计理想投影，并通过两阶段优化分离固定和动态条纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测器响应非均匀性在光子计数探测器CT中引入系统性投影误差和环状伪影，现有固定偏置模型无法充分捕捉投影角缓慢变化的残余条纹幅度，需要更有效的校正方法。

Method: TRACE将条纹表示为固定偏置加低阶DCT分量的组合，使用少量系数描述每个探测器单元的角变化。采用可学习的分析-综合架构表示理想投影，两阶段优化先分离固定条纹再分离动态条纹。角梯度软正交约束抑制共享DCT梯度子空间内的相关变化，减少结构信息泄露到伪影估计中。所有参数直接在实测正弦图上优化，无需配对训练数据。

Result: 在QRM小鼠 phantom和猪蹄实测数据上的实验表明，TRACE在抑制环状伪影和提高图像均匀性的同时，能够保留边缘锐度、软组织纹理和骨小梁细节。

Conclusion: TRACE是一种有效的光子计数探测器CT响应相关误差校正方法，能够在无监督情况下改善图像质量并保留关键解剖结构细节。

Abstract: Detector response nonuniformity introduces systematic projection errors and ring artifacts in photon-counting detector computed tomography (PCD-CT). In measured PCD-CT data, residual stripe amplitudes vary slowly with projection angle, which fixed-bias models cannot adequately capture. We propose TRACE, a two-stage unsupervised sinogram decomposition method for estimating and correcting these response-related errors. TRACE represents stripes as a fixed bias plus low-order discrete cosine transform (DCT) components, using a small number of coefficients to describe angular variations at each detector element. A learnable analysis--synthesis architecture represents the ideal projections, while two-stage optimization separates them from fixed and then dynamic stripes. An angular-gradient soft orthogonality constraint suppresses correlated variations within the shared DCT gradient subspace, reducing the leakage of object structures into the artifact estimate. All parameters are optimized directly on the measured sinogram without paired training data. Experiments on measured QRM mouse phantom and porcine trotter data show that TRACE suppresses ring artifacts and improves image uniformity while preserving edge sharpness, soft-tissue texture, and trabecular detail.

</details>


### [195] [LynnReal-Omni: Native multi-modal Video Generation for Agentic Visual Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15863)
*Xiaofeng Mao,Peijia Lin,Shaohao Rui,Yibo Zhang,Haibin Wan,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LynnReal-Omni是一个统一的原生多模态视频生成框架，基于32B共享多模态扩散Transformer，支持文本到视频、图像条件生成、参考引导生成、结构控制、编辑、降质视频修复和长视频生成。它还训练了一个27B的Flash版本用于实时渲染，在一块H100上生成22帧540p视频仅需843ms（LynnReal-Omni）和377ms（Flash）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型是随机的且难以控制：精确内容通常需要重复采样且不保证成功，长时间场景在外观、交互和时间一致性上会漂移。智能体视觉创作提供明确的参考、可编辑的3D场景或可执行的游戏状态来实现稳定控制，但本身无法保证高对象或角色保真度。将两者结合可以实现稳定、高质量的生成。

Method: 1）构建32B共享多模态扩散Transformer，统一多种视频生成任务；2）训练27B Flash版本用于实时渲染；3）建立系统性数据管道，包括视频清洁、主体关联、多模态标注和对齐控制构建；4）提出MSAVP评估基准，包含100个提示词和20个指标。

Result: LynnReal-Omni支持异构视觉输入（外观参考、可编辑3D渲染、游戏录制），允许智能体在统一模型中组合视觉条件。Flash版本通过模型和解码加速（包括轻量级VAE解码器），在单块H100上22帧540p视频的warm generation和解码仅需843ms（LynnReal-Omni）和377ms（Flash）。

Conclusion: LynnReal-Omni为实时流式视频生成提供了基础，是智能体视觉创作的统一、可控和高效的基础。

Abstract: Video diffusion models are stochastic and hard to control: precise content often requires repeated sampling without guaranteed success, and long-horizon scenes drift in appearance, interactions, and temporal coherence. Agentic visual creation provides explicit references, editable 3D scenes, or executable game states for stable control, but does not by itself guarantee high object or character fidelity. Combining the two can enable stable, high-quality generation. To realize this combination, we present LynnReal-Omni, a native multimodal video generation framework built on a 32B shared multimodal diffusion transformer that unifies text-to-video, image-conditioned generation, reference-guided generation, structural control, editing, degraded video restoration, and long-video generation. It accepts heterogeneous visual inputs, including appearance references, editable 3D renders, and game recordings, allowing agents to compose visual conditions within a unified model. We also train a dedicated 27B Flash shared multimodal diffusion transformer for real-time rendering. We build a systematic data pipeline for video cleaning, subject association, multimodal annotation, and aligned control construction, yielding a curated corpus of multi-shot audiovisual segments, and introduce MSAVP, a 100-prompt, 20-metric evaluation design that separates instruction following, generating plausibility, visual quality, temporal behavior, and audio coordination. LynnReal-Omni-Flash further reduces inference cost through model and decoding acceleration, including a lightweight VAE decoder; on one H100, warm generation and decoding of a 22-frame 540p video take 843 ms with LynnReal-Omni and 377 ms with Flash. These results provide a foundation for real-time streaming video generation, making LynnReal-Omni a unified, controllable, and efficient basis for agentic visual creation.

</details>


### [196] [Anatomical Grounding and Leakage-Aware Multimodal Contrastive Learning for Alzheimer's Disease Classification from Structural MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15888)
*Paul-Gabriel Nicolae,Irina Georgiana Mocanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对阿尔茨海默病深度学习模型中的解剖学无关区域注意和标签泄漏问题进行了系统研究。使用轻量级编码器在ADNI-1数据集上发现：图像分类器常关注颅骨等无关区域；认知评分融合虽达87.3%准确率但存在严重标签泄漏；限制输入至内侧颞叶可提升图像-only性能至65.1%。研究强调了区分泄漏驱动结果与真实影像学结果的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在阿尔茨海默病分期任务中虽然能达到合理准确率，但经常关注解剖学上不相关的区域；同时，加入临床表格的多模态模型常依赖用于分配诊断标签的变量，导致标签泄漏问题。需要系统性地研究这两个问题并提出解决方案。

Method: 采用轻量级切片编码器（ResNet18加一层Transformer）处理ADNI-1的1,075例基线T1加权扫描。使用FastSurfer分割作为解剖参考，训练YOLOv8模型定位阿尔茨海默病相关结构，并使用Grad-CAM比较图像分类器的注意力区域。适配CLIP风格的图像-表格对比框架，将ADNIMERGE变量沿标签泄漏光谱组织。测试了三种融合方式：认知评分融合、区域体积融合、以及限制输入至内侧颞叶区域的策略。

在二分类任务（MCI vs CN）上，对比了将编码器对齐至认知评分（52.4%准确率）与对齐至体积数据（73.8%准确率）的差异。

Result: YOLOv8模型定位阿尔茨海默病相关结构的mAP_50超过0.96。Grad-CAM分析显示图像-only分类器频繁关注颅骨、眼眶和背景区域。认知评分融合的三分类准确率为87.3%（视为泄漏驱动的上限），区域体积融合为73.0%。在MCI vs CN任务中，当编码器对齐至认知评分时准确率为52.4%，对齐至体积数据时为73.8%（推理时均不使用表格输入）。将输入限制至内侧颞叶区域后，图像-only三分类准确率从58.7%提升至65.1%。

Conclusion: 研究强调了阿尔茨海默病分期模型中标签泄漏和解剖相关区域选择的重要性。认知评分融合达到87.3%的准确率应视为泄漏驱动的上限而非影像学结果。限制输入至内侧颞叶区域可将图像-only三分类准确率从58.7%提升至65.1%。所有结果来自小规模平衡测试集的单次运行，需谨慎解读。

该研究的重要价值在于揭示了深度学习模型在AD诊断中的两个关键问题：1) 模型可能学习解剖学上不相关的区域（如颅骨、眼眶）；2) 多模态融合容易受到标签泄漏的影响。提出使用对比学习框架来系统性地评估标签泄漏光谱，为提高模型可解释性和临床可信度提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep networks trained on structural MRI for Alzheimer's disease (AD) staging often reach reasonable accuracy while attending to anatomically irrelevant regions, and multimodal models that add clinical tables frequently rely on variables that were used to assign the diagnostic label in the first place. We study both issues with a deliberately lightweight slice-based encoder (ResNet18 with a one-layer Transformer over slices) on 1,075 baseline T1-weighted scans from ADNI-1. First, we use FastSurfer segmentations as an anatomical reference: YOLOv8 models trained on segmentation-derived labels localize Alzheimer-relevant structures with mAP_50 above 0.96, and a Grad-CAM comparison shows that the image-only classifier frequently attends to the skull, orbits and background. Second, we adapt a CLIP-style image - tabular contrastive framework and organize ADNIMERGE variables along a label-leakage spectrum. Fusion with cognitive scores yields 87.3% three-way accuracy, which we treat as a leakage-driven upper bound rather than an imaging result; fusion with regional volumes yields 73.0%. We observe that the choice of contrastive target changes what the image encoder learns: on MCI vs. CN, the image-only head reaches 52.4% when the encoder is aligned to cognitive scores and 73.8\% when aligned to volumes, although no tabular input is used at inference. Third, restricting the input to a per-subject crop of the medial temporal lobe raises image-only three-way accuracy from 58.7% to 65.1%. All results come from single runs on a small balanced test set, and we report confidence intervals and the protocol differences that prevent direct comparison with published numbers.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [ZGCM-1: A Fully Open and Extremely Efficient Foundation Model for Math and Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13356)
*Jiyan He,Guang Liang,Hao Liu,Haoxiang Guan,Jinbo Sun,Junyi Guo,Wenjun Feng,Yantai Xie,Yifei Shen,Bin Shao,Chuyang Wei,Kai Chen,Kexin Zhou,Minghang Zhu,Shuxin Zheng,Tie-Yan Liu,Taine Zhao,Wenhui Zhu,Xueyin Xu,Xiaoqing Zhang,Yatao Li,Yuxuan Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZGCM-1是一个从零训练的7B紧凑型基础模型，通过将内部思考与外部工具使用相结合来克服参数容量限制。该模型支持256K上下文，采用门控滑动窗口与全注意力混合架构、FP8 Muon优化器，并在16K、64K、256K上下文上实现渐进式课程扩展。模型在多项基准测试中表现具有竞争力，尤其在数学推理和智能体搜索任务上可与规模大得多的前沿模型相媲美，且预训练效率提升约4.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型模型无法被动记忆开放网络，需要通过将内部思考与主动外部工具使用相结合来克服参数容量限制。作者旨在建立一个端到端的高效开放训练方案，支持256K上下文，并建立AI原生的研发工作流程。

Method: 1) 架构与系统协同设计：交织门控滑动窗口与全注意力机制，稳定的FP8 Muon优化器；2) 渐进式课程与MDP中期训练：跨16K、64K、256K的上下文扩展，将交互轨迹重构为马尔可夫决策过程；3) 智能体集群自主管理集群运维、数据筛选和快速诊断评估。

Result: ZGCM-1-7B在7B模型家族的一般基准测试中具有竞争力；在数学推理和智能体搜索等挑战性任务上，与规模大一个数量级的前沿模型（如Qwen3-235B-A22B和GLM-5.1）相当；16K预训练时间到Loss的效率提升约4.2倍。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了紧凑模型通过内部思考与外部工具结合可克服容量限制，提出的训练方法实现了显著效率提升，为社区提供了完整的开源资源（模型权重、训练代码、数据配方等），并总结了八个可操作的实证经验。

Abstract: In this work, we present ZGCM-1, a fully open 7B dense foundation model trained from scratch with extreme data, system, and algorithmic efficiency. ZGCM-1 is founded on a core premise: compact models cannot passively memorize the open web, but can overcome parametric capacity limits by coupling deliberate internal thinking with active external tool use. To support this paradigm across a 256K context, we develop an end-to-end, high-efficiency open training recipe: Architecture & System Co-design: interleaved gated sliding-window and full attention, and a stable FP8 Muon optimizer; Progressive Curriculum & MDP Mid-Training: context scaling across 16K, 64K, and 256K, and the reformulation of interaction traces into Markov Decision Processes. Furthermore, we establish an AI-native R&D workflow where agent swarms autonomously manage cluster operations, data curation, and rapid diagnostic evaluation. Extensive evaluations show that ZGCM-1-7B is competitive across 7B model family on general benchmarks. On several challenging mathematical reasoning and agentic search suites, it remains competitive with frontier models orders of magnitude larger, such as Qwen3-235B-A22B and GLM-5.1. We also show that our pre-training design offers a ~4.2x efficiency improvement in 16K pre-training time-to-loss. Across the full development lifecycle, we distill eight actionable empirical findings-spanning architectural scaling, SFT quality pruning, long-context generalization, and agentic co-training dynamics. To facilitate community research, we open-source model weights from the pre-training, mid-training, and post-training stages, intermediate checkpoints, training code, per-stage data and data recipes, and W&B logs.

</details>


### [198] [Converge Then Diversify: Decoupling Convergence and Diversity in Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13396)
*Chao Jiang,Yueling Huang,Miqing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CTD通过解耦收敛和多样性两个阶段来解决有限预算下MOBO的性能问题，在各种测试中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOBO方法同时追求收敛性和多样性，在有限预算下难以有效工作。当评估预算非常紧张时，无法同时逼近整个帕累托前沿。

Method: 提出先收敛后多样化(CTD)方法，分为两阶段：第一阶段专注于收敛，快速逼近帕累托前沿的单个点；第二阶段专注于多样 性，在整个前沿上分散解决方案。

Result: 在446次成对比较中，CTD在72.9%的情况下统计上优于最先进方法，21.1%表现相当，6.1%统计上较差。该优势在有限预算或高维问题设置中尤为明显。

Conclusion: CTD方法在多目标贝叶斯优化中，特别是在有限评估预算或高维问题场景下，能够有效提升性能和解决方案质量。

Abstract: Multi-objective Bayesian optimisation (MOBO) is a sample-efficient approach for optimising expensive black-box functions with multiple objectives. In MOBO, the goal is to adequately approximate the Pareto front; that is, to obtain a high-quality solution set with 1) good convergence (closeness to the Pareto front) and 2) good diversity (spread across the Pareto front). Existing MOBO methods typically aim to accomplish these two tasks simultaneously, i.e., driving the search towards the Pareto front while maintaining a diverse set of nondominated solutions, such that the solutions, ideally, can gradually approach the entire front. When sufficient search budgets are available, this approach is effective. However, considering both convergence and diversity throughout the search is not easy and requires careful design. Under very tight budgets, there may not be enough solutions generated to be able to simultaneously approach the entire Pareto front. To address this issue, this paper proposes a \textit{{converge-then-diversify}} (CTD) approach that decouples convergence and diversity into two stages. In the first stage, CTD focuses on convergence, aiming to quickly drive the search toward a single point on the Pareto front. In the second stage, CTD focuses on diversity, aiming to spread solutions across the front. We present two simple instantiations of CTD by using widely adopted acquisition functions in the area. Experimental results show that, across all 446 pairwise comparisons, CTD statistically outperforms state-of-the-art methods in 72.9\% of the cases, performs equivalently in 21.1\%, and is statistically worse in only 6.1\%, with the advantage being particularly evident in settings with very tight evaluation budgets or in high-dimensional problems.

</details>


### [199] [Generalized Agent Iteration: One Formal Framework for Iterative Policy Improvement and Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13406)
*Hongyao Tang,Yi Ma,Pengyi Li,Yifu Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出广义智能体迭代(GAI)框架，将迭代策略改进和递归自我改进统一为单一学习范式的两个案例，通过两个关键维度区分：改进机制是否属于智能体一部分，以及评估标准是否外部锚定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前递归自我改进(RSI)在多个尺度被声称存在，但缺乏正式描述这些新兴实例的统一框架；经典领域的迭代策略改进虽有好理解的理论性质，但更新原则和评估基础都位于智能体外部。

Method: 将智能体定义为系统内可修改组件的配置，将学习过程建模为智能体评估与改进的循环；通过两个"拨盘"区分不同实例：改进机制是否属于智能体、评估标准是否外部锚定。

Result: 建立统一框架将现有系统置于同一坐标轴上，使递归自我改进的缺陷可逐一表述；区分了GPI与RSI的边界，确定系统极性为锚定型、目标漂移型或完全自引型。

Conclusion: 这是探索RSI形式化表征的第一步，建立在经典理论基础之上，使现有系统可比较，为分析和设计新系统提供原则性基础。

Abstract: When we speak of recursive self-improvement (RSI), are we speaking of a phenomenon, a mechanism, or a prospect? Towards autonomous and evolving intelligence, RSI is being claimed at many scales, while no single framework that formally describes these emerging instances exists. Its counterpart in the classical realm, iterative policy improvement, is characterized by generalized policy iteration (GPI), a framework of broad applicability with well-understood theoretical properties, but only where the update principle and the evaluation base lie outside the agent. In this paper, we propose Generalized Agent Iteration (GAI), a formal framework that describes iterative policy improvement and RSI as two cases of a single learning paradigm. GAI defines the agent as a configuration of modifiable components within a system and models the learning process as a cycle of agent evaluation and agent improvement. Two pivotal dials then distinguish the instances: whether the improving mechanism is part of the agent and whether the standard it is measured against is grounded outside it. The former dial delineates the boundary between GPI and RSI, and the latter determines a system's polarity as anchored, goal drift, or fully self-referential. Moreover, we use these coordinates to place existing systems on the same two axes and make the defects of recursive self-improvement statable one condition at a time. We see this paper as a first step toward exploring a formal characterization of RSI that rests on the classical account, makes existing systems comparable, and provides a principled basis for analyzing and designing new ones.

</details>


### [200] [Vibe Patenting: Evaluating LLM Judges for Professional Patent-Drafting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13422)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Vlad Blaykhman,Ye Wang,Jing Liu,Gene V. Vinokur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM评判员在复杂专业工作（如AI专利起草）中的可靠性问题，通过Vibe Patenting测试平台评估LLMjudge引导的迭代修订效果，发现judge引导能持续提升质量，使低推理能力智能体接近高推理能力智能体的表现，但judge与专业专利律师评估之间存在系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判员越来越多地被用于评估和改进AI生成内容，但它们在复杂专业工作中的可靠性尚不明确，需要系统研究。

Method: 通过Vibe Patenting端到端专利起草测试平台，使用独立调用的LLM judge评估生成的专利草案并提供结构化反馈进行迭代修订，跨多个发明和起草智能体配置进行实验，并与专业专利律师的独立评估进行对比验证。

Result: 1)judge引导的迭代修订持续提升judge评估质量，无引导修订趋于饱和；2)迭代judge反馈使低推理智能体接近高推理智能体的表现；3)更强的模型和增加推理一般会提高judge评估的起草质量；4)领域特定的智能体工作流提供额外增益；5)与专业专利律师评估对比，存在有意义但高度依赖指标的一致性和系统性校准差异。

Conclusion: LLM评判员作为复杂专业工作流的评估和优化信号既有实用性也有局限性，需要谨慎使用并注意其系统性偏差。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly used to evaluate and improve AI-generated outputs, yet their reliability for complex professional work remains unclear. We study this problem through Vibe Patenting, an end-to-end patent-drafting testbed for AI-agent evaluation. A separately-invoked LLM judge evaluates generated patent drafts and provides structured feedback for iterative revision. Across multiple inventions and drafting-agent configurations, judge-guided revision consistently improves judge-assessed quality, while unguided revision tends to saturate. Notably, iterative judge feedback enables a low-reasoning agent to approach the performance of a substantially more expensive high-reasoning agent. Stronger models and increased reasoning generally improve judge-assessed drafting quality, while domain-specific agentic workflows provide further gains. We validate the judge against independent evaluation by a professional patent attorney and find meaningful but strongly metric-dependent agreement and systematic calibration differences. These results highlight both the utility and limitations of LLM judges as evaluators and optimization signals for complex professional workflows.

</details>


### [201] [Toward Self-Adaptive Physical AI: Can LLM Agents Manage Long-Horizon Physical Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13436)
*Varun Kaushik,Yayun Tan,Xiaofan Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究零样本LLM代理在长期物理任务中的自适应性，无需人类干预即可适应环境变化，在农业任务中与强化学习代理进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理任务代理需要大量数据重新训练，且主要在虚拟世界中运行，缺乏在真实物理环境中无需人类干预即可自适应的能力。

Method: 设计多代理框架，整合规划、工具调用、观察和验证四个模块，在农业场景中进行测试。

Result: 零样本LLM代理在相同天气模式下可达到与RL代理相当的管理成果，且在环境变化时比RL代理具有更好的适应性。

Conclusion: 零样本LLM代理为构建自适应性物理AI代理提供了可行路径，有望应用于农业等长期物理任务管理。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer a promising path toward autonomously managing long-term physical tasks without human intervention. However, physical tasks require agents to continuously observe the environment, make consequential actions, and remain effective as the environment changes. Existing approaches either require substantial data and retraining, or primarily focus on agents operating in the virtual world. In this work, we explore the feasibility of building a self-adaptive physical AI agent that manages long-term physical tasks in a zero-shot manner and adapts to environmental changes without human intervention. We design a multi-agent framework that integrates planning, tool calling, observation, and verification, and evaluate it on agricultural tasks against reinforcement learning (RL) agents under different weather patterns. Our results show that zero-shot LLM agents can achieve comparable management outcomes to RL agents under the same weather pattern and adapt more effectively than RL when evaluated under a shifted environment, highlighting a promising path toward self-adaptive physical AI agents.

</details>


### [202] [A Hybrid Agentic AI Framework for Intelligent Supply Chain Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13561)
*Xian Yeow Lee,Teppei Inoue,Haiyan Wang,Chetan Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于供应链分析的多智能体系统，通过协调智能体解释用户意图并委托给专业智能体，桥接商业决策与技术专业知识之间的鸿沟。该系统在多级库存管理测试环境中达到90%准确率，与单智能体基线相当，但输入token使用量减少约四倍，显著提高了可扩展性和成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链分析的有效利用仍然是规划者的重大挑战，因为数据库查询、关键绩效指标(KPI)分析、需求预测和性能诊断等关键任务需要跨越数据工程、运筹学和领域知识的异构专业知识。现有的单一方法难以同时支持探索性分析和确定性工作流程。

Method: 提出一种智能体系统架构，包含一个协调智能体（解释用户意图并委托子任务）和多个专业智能体（处理特定领域任务）。系统支持探索性分析和确定性工作流程的转换，领域逻辑封装在专业智能体和提示中，实现可扩展、模块化、可审计的设计。

Result: 在复制多级库存管理操作的测试环境中评估，多智能体设计达到90%准确率，与单智能体基线相当，同时将输入token使用量减少约四倍，大幅提升可扩展性和成本效益。此外，还展示了可解释的次优检测和自动预测优化的案例研究。

Conclusion: 智能体架构可以有效结合开放式的探索性分析和确定性的供应链分析工作流程，为更易访问和可扩展的决策支持系统提供了一条实用路径。提示中心开发方式降低了功能扩展成本。

Abstract: Efficient utilization of supply chain analytics for decision making remains a significant challenge for planners, as critical tasks such as database querying, key performance indicator (KPI) analysis, demand forecasting, and performance diagnosis require heterogeneous expertise spanning data engineering, operations research, and domain knowledge. In this work, we propose an agentic system for supply chain analytics that bridges the gap between business decision-making and technical expertise, where a coordinator agent interprets user intent and delegates sub-tasks to specialized agents. The system supports both exploratory analysis and deterministic workflows, enabling planners to transition between ad hoc questions and structured processes. Domain logic is encapsulated within specialist agents and prompts, yielding a scalable, modular, and auditable design and lowering the cost of functional extension through prompt-centric development. We evaluate the proposed architecture on a test environment that replicates multi-echelon inventory management operations. Results show that our multi-agent design achieves a 90\% accuracy, which is competitive with a single agent baseline while reducing input token usage by roughly fourfold, substantially improving scalability and cost-efficiency. Furthermore, we provide case studies to demonstrate interpretable suboptimality detection and automated forecast optimization, illustrating how agentic architectures can effectively combine open-ended exploratory analysis and deterministic supply chain analytics workflows, and provide a practical pathway toward more accessible and extensible decision-support systems.

</details>


### [203] [LabAgent: Customize Any Research Hubs for Scientific Discoveries Using AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13437)
*Lei Liu,Yikun Zhang,Jialin Chen,Wanjia Zhao,Rex Ying,Wengong Jin,Hua Xu,James Zou,Tianyu Liu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对实验室人员流动导致方法无法传承的问题，提出了LabAgent——一个用于实验室持续工作的复现与发现框架，通过两种机制确保所有技能可执行验证，并记录纠正方法和经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研工作中，前辈开发的方法常因学生毕业等因素导致人员变动，使得方法无法被传承和复现，已投入大量资源和精力的方法无法继续。

Method: 提出LabAgent框架，采用两种机制：1) 确保所有技能可执行和验证；2) 记录纠正方法和经验教训，用于直接纠正或避免类似错误。

Result: 在药物性质预测、生物医学问题分析、蛋白质变异效应预测和统计遗传学四个生命科学领域进行应用，LabAgent在每个领域的表现均优于商业通用智能体，并成功复现了已发表论文中的图表。

Conclusion: LabAgent能够有效整合并合理扩展实验室知识，为实验室的持续性工作提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Scientific research is a continuous process that emphasizes inheritance. Methods developed by predecessors are often expanded upon by new researchers to explore more novel and in-depth scientific questions. However, the change of lab staff, such as student graduation, leads to a lack of personnel capable of replicating methods. Methods that have been developed with significant effort and resources cannot be continued. To address these limitations, we propose LabAgent, a reproduce and discovery harness tailored for a lab's continuous work. LabAgent employs two mechanisms to guarantee that all skills can be executed and verified and to record the corrective methods and experiences, allowing for direct correction or avoidance of similar errors. We applied LabAgent to drug property prediction, biomedical problem analysis, protein variant effect prediction, and statistical genetics in life science domains. LabAgent ranks first over commercial generalist agents in every domain, and demonstrates accurate reproduction of a published figure. Overall, these results demonstrate that LabAgent can effectively integrate and reasonably expand laboratory knowledge.

</details>


### [204] [Identity Is More Than Recall: A Benchmark for Persistent Identity in Deployed AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13637)
*Zhenyu Zhao,Roy Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAI-Bench是一个_provider-neutral_基准测试,用于评估持久代理对版本化、更新治理的身份合约的忠诚度。它将回忆、组合、行为 enactment、抵抗、持久性、血统和角色条件更新分开,同时将评分预言排除在目标流程之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久代理需要评估来区分它们可以回忆的身份事实与它们表达和 enacting 的身份事实。现有评估未能区分这些不同的身份方面。

Method: 两个冻结活动涵盖16个合成配置文件、32个探针和3个独立初始化的目标配置,产生1,536个保留响应。进行了法官独立的_literal audit_、字段提示实验、启动体标签替换实验。

Result: 直接父标识符在48/48个原子响应中被发现,但仅在1/48个隐式自画像中被发现。显式字段提示将三个身份标识符的联合存在从0/8增加到7/8。启动体标签替换将全指定存在从1/8增加到7/8。Claude平均得分比Astra低12.5个百分点。

Conclusion: PAI-Bench提供了一个可重现的评估协议,可将身份合约忠诚度的三个方面——事实可用性、身份表达和行为 enactment——作为distinct aspects进行测量。揭示了提示依赖的组件选择和启动线索的组件特异性敏感性。

Abstract: Persistent agents need evaluations that distinguish identity facts they can recall from those they express and enact. We introduce PAI-Bench, a provider-neutral benchmark for fidelity to a versioned, update-governed identity contract. It separates recall, composition, behavioral enactment, resistance, persistence, lineage, and role-conditioned updates while keeping scoring oracles outside the target process. Two frozen campaigns cover sixteen synthetic profiles, thirty-two probes, and three independently initialized target configurations, yielding 1,536 retained responses. A judge-independent literal audit finds direct-parent identifiers in 48/48 atomic responses but only 1/48 implicit self-portraits. On eight profiles, explicit field cues increase joint presence of three identity identifiers from 0/8 to 7/8 under the same four-sentence instruction. A separate startup body-label substitution increases full-designation presence from 1/8 to 7/8 while parents remain absent. These contrasts reveal prompt-dependent component selection and component-specific sensitivity to startup cues in the tested deployments. Replaying identical factorial responses also yields a Claude headline mean 12.5 percentage points below Astra's, demonstrating evaluator sensitivity separately from target behavior. The studies use single target samples per condition, with post-hoc audits and follow-ups. PAI-Bench provides a reproducible evaluation protocol for measuring factual availability, identity expression, and behavioral enactment as distinct aspects of identity-contract fidelity.

</details>


### [205] [TimeThink: Eliciting Compositional Reasoning in Timeseries Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13457)
*Sudarshan Regmi,Arvind Pillai,Yu Yvonne Wu,Yuliang Chen,Bibek Panthi,Tess Z. Griffin,Michael V. Heinz,Lisa Marsch,Nicholas C. Jacobson,Andrew Campbell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeThink是一个合成框架，旨在通过强化学习与可验证奖励(RLVR)训练策略，激发时间序列多模态大语言模型的组合推理能力。该框架利用领域无关的时间序列原语（如趋势、季节性）生成合成数据，使模型能够学习组合逻辑而非仅仅模仿推理痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列多模态大语言模型(TS-MLLMs)无法捕捉动态时间模式，仅提供隐式推理，缺乏高风险应用（如医疗）所需的关键解释；基于强化学习的时间序列语言模型虽然在窄分布内训练数据上表现尚可，但难以处理分布外的组合问题。

Method: 1)设计合成数据生成器，产生原子和复合问答对，提供带推理轨迹的客观真值；2)采用强化学习与可验证奖励(RLVR)训练策略，鼓励显式推理；3)核心时间序列原语（趋势、季节性等）具有领域独立性，可确定性生成。

Result: TimeThink仅在合成数据上训练，就在合成和真实世界基准测试中显著优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: TimeThink通过合成数据和RLVR训练，使模型能够学习时间序列组合的底层逻辑，而非依赖模板，为高风险应用提供可解释的显式推理能力。

Abstract: Timeseries multimodal large language models (TS-MLLMs) have recently begun leveraging the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) for question-answering tasks. However, these models often fail to capture dynamic temporal patterns, providing only implicit reasoning that lacks the underlying explanations critical for high-stakes applications like healthcare. While reinforcement learning (RL)-based timeseries language models aim to address this, they often fall short because they are trained on narrow, in-distribution data and struggle with out-of-distribution compositional questions. To address these challenges, we present TimeThink, a synthetic framework for eliciting compositional timeseries reasoning. Core timeseries primitives (e.g., trend, seasonality) are domain-independent and can be deterministically generated. Guided by this premise, TimeThink first designs a synthetic data generator that produces atomic and composite question-answer pairs, providing objective ground truth with reasoning traces.
