A training-side validation gap-correction diagnostic recovers the point-estimate deployable sign on all four sign-changing cells; it is a reporting aid, not a substitute for direct end-to-end evaluation. Partition and end-to-end scores should be reported side by side.

</details>


### [241] [Do Not Restart: Residual Completion for Stateful Agent Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13800)
*Runzhi Deng,Yiming Zhong,Fang Zhao,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CFRC框架，通过在模型间传递控制来降低工具代理成本，同时保留已接受的选择、已实现的效果和未完成的义务。该方法在五个环境和两组同提供商模型对上实现了与完整任务代理相当的宏观准确率，但推理成本仅为22.0%-34.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路由和级联通过跨模型转移控制来降低工具代理成本，但状态交接必须保留已接受的选择、已实现的效果和未完成的义务。这一问题需要系统性地处理。

Method: 提出承诺-边界残差完成(CFRC)，强制执行目标-先于-提案、整体提案-先于-权威、活证据-先于-成功：从已接受进度中冻结残差合同，将后继延续闭合为证据链接图，仅当剩余部分被覆盖时才允许执行，用活收据解除义务。

Result: 建立了合同相对部分正确性，扩展到完整合同构建下的原始残差请求。在五个环境和两组同提供商模型对上，CFRC以仅22.0%-34.6%的推理成本实现了与强完整任务代理相当的宏观准确率，跨提供商结果进一步证明了更广泛的迁移性。

Conclusion: CFRC成功实现了模型间高效控制转移，在保持准确率的同时显著降低了推理成本，为跨模型状态传递提供了理论保证和实践验证。

Abstract: Routing and cascades reduce tool-agent cost by transferring control across models, but stateful handoffs must preserve accepted choices, realized effects, and unfinished obligations. We formulate this as commitment-constrained residual completion and introduce Commitment-Frontier Residual Completion (CFRC). CFRC enforces target-before-proposal, whole-proposal-before-authority, and live-evidence-before-success: it freezes a residual contract from accepted progress, closes the successor continuation into an evidence-linked graph, and admits execution only when the remainder is covered, with live receipts discharging obligations. We establish contract-relative partial correctness, which extends to the original residual request under complete contract construction. Across five environments and two same-provider model pairs, CFRC achieves comparable macro accuracy to strong full-task agents at only 22.0%-34.6% of their inference cost, with additional cross-provider results demonstrating broader transfer.

</details>


### [242] [LLM-Enhanced Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Unified Electric Vehicles-Charging Station-Grid Optimization in Public Charging Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13805)
*Yang Zhang,Lindong Xie,Chongyu Wang,Gaojunjie Li,Siqi Bu,Edward Chung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM增强的多智能体强化学习(MARL)框架，用于优化物联网环境下的电动汽车充电调度，首次在统一框架中同时优化电网、EV用户和充电站三方利益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网时代下，电动汽车充电调度面临多重目标冲突（用户满意度、充电站盈利、电网稳定性）的挑战，现有MARL方法难以处理高维状态空间且无法有效平衡多方利益相关者的冲突目标。

Method: 提出LLM增强的MARL框架，利用LLM分析实时IoT环境状态，提取物理显著特征，并通过语义推理动态分配冲突目标（利润、用户满意度、电网负载）的权重，实现自适应多目标平衡。

Result: 实验表明，该框架显著优于现有最先进方法，市场效率大幅提升，训练时间减少超过70%。

Conclusion: 该方法为高效可持续的物联网城市充电基础设施管理提供了可扩展、透明的技术解决方案。

Abstract: In the era of the Internet of Things (IoT), coordinating connected electric vehicle (EV) charging scheduling to balance EV charging satisfaction, station profitability, and smart grid stability presents a complex multi-objective challenge. Existing Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) approaches often struggle with high-dimensional state spaces generated by massive IoT sensing data and conflicting stakeholder interests. This paper proposes a novel LLM-enhanced MARL framework that, for the first time, simultaneously optimizes the Grid, EVs, and Stations within a unified loop. By integrating Large Language Model (LLM), we address two critical bottlenecks: interpretable feature selection and adaptive multi-objective balancing. The LLM analyzes real-time IoT-collected environmental states to extract physically significant features and dynamically assigns weights to conflicting objectives-including profit, user satisfaction, and grid load-using semantic reasoning instead of complex manual tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving superior market efficiency while reducing training time by over 70%. This approach offers a scalable, transparent solution for efficient and sustainable IoT-enabled urban charging infrastructure management.

</details>


### [243] [Bypass Observation: A Conceptual Design of a Non-Intrusive Layer-Wise Semantic Extraction Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13807)
*Haibin Tong,Jiang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bypass Observation（非侵入式分层读出架构），通过在Transformer选定层附加只读观察头来实现对高层语言模型隐藏状态的观测，而无需将输出反馈回主干网络。该架构包含三种变体：跨层共享头、层特定头和层/步自适应头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在高维隐藏状态空间中进行推理，但用户只能观察最终输出。这种不透明性限制了模型的可解释性和可观测性。传统链式思考（CoT）虽然引入了中间推理 token，但会进入自回归计算；作者希望提出一种因果外部的观察机制。

Method: 提出三种Bypass Observation变体：(1) 跨层共享LM头；(2) 层特定头；(3) 层/步自适应头。通过完整词汇表读出，推导出闭式开销近似公式V/(12d)，开销范围约30%-240%，并讨论了稀疏观察、低秩分解、减少词汇表、top-k读出和选择性位置等成本降低策略。

Result: 推导出全词汇表读出的计算开销主要受V/(12d)governed，其中V为词汇表大小，d为隐藏维度。开销范围约30%-240%。此外讨论了Bypass CoT与传统CoT的关键区别：传统CoT推理token进入自回归计算，而bypass读出在推理时保持因果外部性，但可作为强化学习的训练信号。

Conclusion: Bypass Observation可增强模型计算的可观测性，同时保持对隐藏状态的局部、潜在误导性的投影。该方法尤其适用于循环Transformers和循环深度Transformers，可暴露迭代收敛、振荡和潜在停止信号。论文为概念性和分析性工作，系统性实证验证为未来工作。

Abstract: Large language models reason in high-dimensional hidden-state spaces, while users observe only final outputs. We introduce Bypass Observation, a non-intrusive layer-wise readout architecture that attaches read-only observation heads to selected Transformer layers without feeding their outputs back into the backbone. We consider three variants: a shared LM head across layers, layer-specific heads, and a layer- or step-adaptive head. For full-vocabulary readout, we derive a closed-form overhead approximation governed primarily by V/(12d), with representative estimates ranging from about 30% to 240%, and discuss cost reductions via sparse observation, low-rank factorization, reduced vocabularies, top-k readout, and selective positions. We argue that Bypass Observation can make model computation more observable while remaining only a partial, potentially misleading projection of hidden states. We further distinguish bypass chain-of-thought from conventional chain-of-thought: conventional reasoning tokens enter the autoregressive computation, whereas bypass readouts remain causally external at inference time, although they can still provide training signals in reinforcement learning. Finally, we discuss applications to looped and recurrent-depth Transformers, where iteration-wise readout may expose convergence, oscillation, and potential halting signals. The proposal is conceptual and analytical; systematic empirical validation remains future work.

</details>


### [244] [ViperQ: Order Flow Pattern Recognition via Auction Market Theory for Reinforcement Learning Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13825)
*Asser Moustafa,Rares-Mihail Neagu,Jugal Kalita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ViperQ是一种基于强化学习的交易系统，其状态表示完全基于拍卖市场理论(AMT)原语构建（成交量控制点、价值区域位置、低成交量节点、累积成交量Delta分歧和交易速度特征），结合PPO算法和基于前景理论的非对称奖励函数，在TSLA和NVDA上分别实现+163.6%和+116.5%的ROI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术文献中的强化学习交易系统主要依赖价格聚合状态表示(OHLCV)或限价订单簿深度特征，忽略了来自实践者文献的微观结构模式理论——即拍卖市场理论和市场概况——缺乏同行评审的计算实例化。

Method: 1.状态表示：基于AMT原语构建20维Z标准化向量，包含成交量控制点、价值区域位置、低成交量节点、累积成交量Delta分歧和交易速度特征；2.使用两个近端策略优化(PPO)智能体；3.采用基于前景理论的不对称奖励函数，按照Kahneman和Tversky的损失厌恶系数对亏损持仓进行惩罚。

Result: 在机构级tick数据的12个月留出测试集上，TSLA实现+163.6% ROI（最大回撤-27.5%，27,019笔交易），NVDA实现+116.5% ROI（最大回撤-47.8%，12,892笔交易），零杠杆条件下。

Conclusion: 拍卖市场理论特征可作为金融时间序列序贯决策的可行结构化输入模态，为微观结构感知策略学习的进一步研究提供了依据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning trading systems published in the academic literature overwhelmingly rely on price-aggregate state representations (OHLCV bars) or limit-order-book depth features, leaving microstructure pattern theories from the practitioner literature, namely Auction Market Theory and Market Profile, without a peer-reviewed computational instantiation. We present ViperQ, a reinforcement learning system whose state representation is built explicitly from Auction Market Theory primitives: Volume Point of Control, Value Area position, Low Volume Node flags, Cumulative Volume Delta divergence, and tape-velocity signatures, assembled into a 20-dimensional Z-normalised vector. Two Proximal Policy Optimisation agents are trained with a prospect theory-grounded asymmetric reward function that penalises losing holds at a magnitude consistent with Kahneman and Tversky's loss-aversion coefficient. Evaluated on a held-out twelve-month partition of institutional tick data the agents have never seen, ViperQ achieves +163.6% ROI on TSLA (-27.5% max drawdown, 27,019 trades) and +116.5% ROI on NVDA (-47.8% max drawdown, 12,892 trades) under zero leverage. The results establish Auction Market Theory features as a tractable structured input modality for sequential decision-making on financial time series and motivate further work on microstructure-aware policy learning.

</details>


### [245] [UniCAR-RL: Seeing Better before Thinking Deeper in Visual Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13849)
*Yuzhe Li,Hao Yan,Hao Wang,Xingchen Liu,Ya-Qi Yu,Jihao Wu,Minghui Liao,Wei Chen,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UniCAR-RL框架，通过解耦感知和推理的优化来提升多模态大语言模型的数学视觉推理能力。该框架包含三个协同分支：Caption-RL、Reasoning-RL和QA-RL，在无需人工标注的情况下显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. MLLMs在复杂数学视觉推理任务中表现不佳，根源在于细粒度感知能力不足
2. 初始视觉幻觉直接触发级联推理失败
3. 传统端到端强化学习中稀疏奖励无法解耦感知幻觉和逻辑错误
4. 基于感知增强CoT数据的微调成本高且容易引入幻觉

Method: UniCAR-RL框架包含三个协同分支：
1. Caption-RL分支：通过验证器引导的推理验证优化感知能力
2. Reasoning-RL分支：基于黄金图像描述执行逻辑推理，阻止错误级联
3. QA-RL分支：保留原生端到端对齐，确保稳健的问答性能
该框架实现感知与推理能力的隔离和独立优化。

Result: 实验表明UniCAR-RL仅使用原始短答案数据就能大幅提升MLLMs的数学和视觉推理能力，且在不同的模型架构和规模上展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniCAR-RL通过解耦感知与推理优化的创新范式，成功解决了MLLMs在数学视觉推理中的核心挑战。该方法无需人工标注数据，在多个架构和规模上证明了有效性，为提升多模态推理能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle with complex mathematical visual reasoning primarily due to a lack of fine-grained perception, causing initial visual hallucinations to directly trigger cascading reasoning failures. In traditional end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL), sparse rewards fail to decouple perceptual hallucinations from logical missteps, hindering targeted perception optimization. Alternatively, fine-tuning with perception-enhanced CoT data incurs high costs and hallucinations. In this paper, we address these challenges by proposing UniCAR-RL, an annotation-free RL framework. By explicitly decoupling the optimization of perception and reasoning during the training process, it achieves isolation and optimization of both capabilities. Specifically, UniCAR-RL consists of three synergistic branches: 1) a Caption-RL branch that optimizes perception capabilities through verifier-guided reasoning validation; 2) a Reasoning-RL branch that performs logical reasoning based on a gold image description to halt cascading errors; 3) a QA-RL branch that retains native end-to-end alignment to ensure robust question-answering performance. Experiments show that UniCAR-RL substantially improves MLLMs' mathematical and visual reasoning using only raw short-answer data. Furthermore, it demonstrates strong generalization across diverse architectures and scales.

</details>


### [246] [ClinAgent: A ReAct-Based Agent for Conversational Access to Clinical Trial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13860)
*Antonino Vaccarella,Riccardo Cantini,Domenico Talia,Paolo Trunfio,Marianna Talia,Rosamaria Lappano,Marcello Maggiolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClinAgent是一个基于智能体检索增强生成(RAG)的对话系统，使临床医生和研究人员能够用自然语言查询临床试验信息，在多轮交互中获得基于最新数据的回答。系统采用ReAct范式的LLM智能体，集成ClinicalTrials.gov搜索、PubMed模块和本地结构化数据分析器。评估显示Gemini 3.0 Flash整体表现最佳，DeepSeek(思考模式)在规划质量上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前查询临床试验注册库仍依赖手工操作，过程繁琐且容易出错。研究人员需要处理大量半结构化数据，但缺乏自然语言交互支持或跨来源综合能力。

Method: 采用三阶段评估框架，从操作有效性、规划质量、工具使用效率和专家定性判断四个维度评估系统性能。对比三种LLM后端：Gemini 3.0 Flash和两个DeepSeek V3.2变体(思考模式和非思考模式)。

Result: DeepSeek(思考模式)在规划质量上表现最佳，Gemini实现最高整体性能和最佳专家评分。系统成功支持多轮交互中的自然语言查询，并能综合多个数据源返回有依据的回答。

Conclusion: 智能体AI系统有望改善临床试验信息的可访问性和综合能力，支持更高效、以用户为中心的生物医学研究工作流程。

Abstract: Querying clinical trial registries remains a manual and error-prone process, requiring researchers to navigate large volumes of semi-structured data without support for natural language interaction or cross-source synthesis. To address this, we introduce ClinAgent, a conversational system based on agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) that enables clinicians and researchers to query clinical trial information in plain language and receive grounded, up-to-date responses across multi-turn interactions. The system centers on a Large Language Model (LLM) agent following the ReAct paradigm, which iteratively reasons over queries, selects among a set of integrated tools, and refines its actions based on intermediate outputs. These tools include a ClinicalTrials.gov search interface, a PubMed module, and a Python-based analyzer operating on a locally cached structured dataset of clinical trials. We evaluate the system using a three-phase framework assessing operational effectiveness, planning quality, tool-use efficiency, and expert qualitative judgments, comparing three LLM backends: Gemini 3.0 Flash and two variants of DeepSeek V3.2 (thinking and non-thinking). Results reveal complementary strengths, with DeepSeek (thinking mode) excelling in planning quality, while Gemini achieves the highest overall performance and strongest expert ratings. Overall, our findings highlight the potential of agentic AI systems to improve the accessibility and synthesis of clinical trial information, supporting more efficient and user-centered biomedical research workflows.

</details>


### [247] [Map Users and Mapmakers: The Scope of Cognitive Attribution from Acquired Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13879)
*Yiling Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个五部分归属表框架，用于区分有效追踪、获得结构的应用、从明确规范中的获得、从识别观察中的获得以及保留和重用，从而界定获得性表征扩展认知能力的范围及其限度。该框架应用于Andrew Ng的Othello-GPT世界模型解释和当代机器概念指标，同时保留已 demonstrated 的识别、分类和推理能力，同时明确区分什么已被确立 versus 什么仍未被确立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决认知归属问题：获得性表征虽然扩展了系统的认知资源，但不一定转移产生该表征的能力。现有讨论往往混淆表征的获得与应用，导致对认知能力的过高评价。本文旨在精确界定这种扩展及其边界。

Method: 构建五部分归属表，区分：(1)有效追踪 (2)获得结构的应用 (3)从明确规范获得 (4)从识别观察获得 (5)保留和重用。每个条目识别一个积极的能力承诺和一个需要额外支持的主张。通过地图和范畴示例说明为何完全应用能力仍无法确定获得能力，以及为何从描述中获得标准不同于从示例中发现。

Result: 框架保留了已论证的识别、分类、推理和限定条件下的获得能力，同时明确界定标准发现和积累方面仍未确立的内容。应用于Othello-GPT和机器概念指标，表明认知归属的范围问题与概念拥有的构成条件是不同的。

Conclusion: 认知成就应归因于其建立的能力，但不应通过附加的标签 silent 引入更广泛的认知资源。关键区分在于：认知归属的范围问题（哪些能力可归因于系统）vs 概念拥有的构成条件（什么构成对概念的真正持有）。

Abstract: An acquired representation can enlarge a system's cognitive repertoire without transferring the capacities exercised in producing that representation. This paper develops a framework for specifying that enlargement and its limits. Its central contribution is a five-part attribution table distinguishing effective tracking, application of acquired structures, acquisition from explicit specifications, acquisition from identifying observations, and retention and reuse. Each entry identifies a positive capacity commitment and a further claim requiring additional support. The argument deliberately grants meaningful content, causal efficacy, and productive inference, so that its conclusion does not depend on treating representations as inert encodings. Map and category examples show why even complete application competence leaves acquisition capacity undetermined, and why acquiring a criterion from its description differs from finding it in examples. Short formal proofs appear in an appendix. The framework is applied to Andrew Ng's world-model interpretation of Othello-GPT and to the specific indicators discussed in contemporary accounts of machine concepts. It preserves demonstrated recognition, classification, inference, and qualified acquisition while specifying what remains unestablished about criterion discovery and accumulation. The result concerns the scope of cognitive attribution rather than the constitutive conditions of concept possession: cognitive achievements deserve credit for the capacities they establish, without silently importing a broader repertoire through the labels attached to them.

</details>


### [248] [LoRA Fine-Tuned Models for Control Systems Course Q\&A: A Multidimensional Evaluation of Model Scale and Rank Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13918)
*Shaowen Lu,Chengxu Liu,Ping Zhou,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究使用LoRA微调Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-7B模型，用于回答线性控制系统课程问题。通过360个系统-用户-助手对话数据集进行监督微调，比较了不同LoRA秩(r=4,8,16)的效果。结果显示LoRA提升了文本相似性和结构化输出稳定性，7B-r16达到最高ROUGE-L(0.4093)和BERTScore-F1(0.8643)，但指标主要衡量文本相似度和格式一致性，而非领域特定推理或数学正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在大学专业课程中应用日益广泛，但控制系统问题需要协调的术语、符号、推导和逐步解释。直接使用通用模型回复结构不一致且难以验证，需要针对课程参考答案的语言和教学组织进行对齐优化。

Method: 使用线性控制系统课程的练习题和参考解决方案，构建了360个系统-用户-助手对话的监督微调数据集。对Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct应用LoRA微调，测试LoRA秩r=4、8、16。采用ROUGE、BERTScore和结构化输出特征进行评估，并通过Bootstrap重采样验证结果稳定性。

Result: LoRA在两个模型规模上都提升了相似性和结构化输出稳定性。7B-r16取得最高ROUGE-L(0.4093)和BERTScore-F1(0.8643)，r=8在性能和参数效率间提供更好平衡。Bootstrap分析显示3B-r16的ROUGE-L增益为0.0764[0.0613,0.0915]，7B-r16为0.0874[0.0687,0.1042]，置信区间均超过零，表明文本相似性改进稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA可以更紧密地将开源指令微调模型与课程参考答案的语言和教学组织对齐。然而，当前指标主要捕捉文本相似性和格式一致性，而非领域特定推理或数学正确性，这需要专家评估和任务特定评分标准。未来需要开发更全面的评估框架来衡量数学推理质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in specialized university courses, but control-systems questions require coordinated terminology, notation, derivations, and stepwise explanations. Direct general-purpose responses may be inconsistently structured and hard to verify. Using exercises and reference solutions from a Linear Control Systems course, we built a supervised fine-tuning dataset of 360 system-user-assistant conversations. We applied LoRA to Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. With identical data splits, inference settings, and evaluation protocols, we compared base and fine-tuned models and tested LoRA ranks r=4, 8, and 16. Evaluation used ROUGE, BERTScore, and structured-output features to measure reference-answer similarity and stability of the Solution-Method-Teaching Points format. LoRA improved both similarity and structured-output stability at both sizes. On the current test set, 7B-r16 achieved the highest ROUGE-L (0.4093) and BERTScore-F1 (0.8643), while r=8 offered a better balance between performance and parameter efficiency. Bootstrap resampling showed ROUGE-L gains of 0.0764 [0.0613, 0.0915] for 3B-r16 and 0.0874 [0.0687, 0.1042] for 7B-r16; both intervals exceeded zero, indicating stable textual-similarity improvements on the current test set. These results suggest LoRA can align open-source instruction-tuned models more closely with the language and pedagogical organization of course reference answers. However, the metrics mainly capture textual similarity and formatting consistency, not domain-specific reasoning or mathematical correctness, which require expert assessment and task-specific rubrics.

</details>


### [249] [SAILOR: Solver-Assisted Interactive LLM-based Optimization Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13945)
*Shaghayegh Sadeghi,Stephen L. Smith,David C. Del Rey Fern'andez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Natural-language descriptions of optimization problems may be incomplete or vague about numerical information that a solver requires, including costs, capacities, demands, bounds, and penalties. A language model can translate the description into code, but when a required value is absent it must either stop or guess. We present SAILOR, a proof-of-concept system that detects such unsupported numerical choices, asks the user targeted follow-up questions, and updates the optimization model before returning a solution. Questions are prioritized using uncertainty and solver-derived estimates of how strongly each missing value affects the current model. We evaluate the pipeline on 1,723 instances from seven masked benchmarks using an idealized simulator that returns ground-truth values. Exact objective-value agreement ranges from 27.0% to 87.6% across datasets, with 1.4--5.7 questions per instance on average. These results establish feasibility under controlled branch-and-reveal feedback; they do not measure performance with human users or general structural model repair. Code is available at: https://github.com/sshaghayeghs/SAILOR.

</details>


### [250] [Synthetic Data in Marketing Research: How to Evaluate and When to Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13995)
*Oded Netzer,Rajan Sambandam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究探讨了合成数据（LLM生成的合成受访者）在营销研究中的应用，驳斥了"完全替代"和"完全排斥"两种极端观点，提出关键问题不是"是否"而是"何时"使用合成受访者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于合成数据的争论两极化，要么声称LLM可完全取代人类受访者，要么主张完全避免使用。作者认为两种立场都忽略了更有价值的问题：合成受访者在何种情境下有效。

Method: 1) 区分三种合成数据类型并映射决策用途；2) 建立四类准确率测量 taxonomy；3) 提出"遗忘问题"情境下的 twin-based augmentation 方法，使用R²诊断（无需真实值）；4) 基于108个态度问题的全国代表性调查（N=3,063）进行验证

Result: R² > 0.7的筛选标准使twin-human个体层面相关性平均提升15%，表现不佳问题的比例从25.9%降至4.3%。嵌入相似性和经验研究者判断提供相关但较弱的筛选能力。

Conclusion: 合成数据并非全有或全无的问题。作者提出的框架帮助研究者判断何时适合使用不同类型的合成数据，以及如何通过R²诊断确保数据质量。

Abstract: Debate over synthetic data in marketing research has polarized between claims that large language models (LLMs) make human respondents obsolete and calls to avoid them entirely. We argue that both positions obscure the more useful question: not whether synthetic respondents work, but when. Building on Brand, Israeli, and Ngwe (2026), we make three contributions. First, we distinguish three types of synthetic data (ungrounded LLM responses, segment-level personas, and individual-level digital twins) and map each to the decisions it can support. Second, we develop a taxonomy of four families of accuracy measures and suggest that the wide range of reported twin accuracy, from near-perfect to near-chance, largely reflects differences in what is being measured rather than in method quality. Aggregate measures often perform well even when little information is supplied to the LLM, and can mask a complete absence of respondent-level differentiation. Third, we introduce the forgotten question problem, in which a question is omitted from a fielded study, as a setting for twin-based augmentation of existing data. We propose an ex-ante answerability diagnostic that requires no ground truth: the R^2 of a random forest predicting twin outputs from the data used to construct the twins. Across 108 attitude questions from a nationally representative survey (N = 3,063), screening at R^2 above 0.7 raises the mean twin-human individual-level correlation by 15% and reduces the share of poorly answered questions from 25.9% to 4.3%. Embedding similarity and experienced-researcher judgment provide correlated but weaker screens.

</details>


### [251] [Convergent Emergence of In-Context Learning Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14011)
*Nathan Breslow,Seungwook Han,Daniel Hyunsoo Lee,Aayush Mishra,Anqi Liu,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Few-shot in-context learning (ICL), the capacity of a model to infer abstract patterns from input-output examples provided in its prompt and apply them to new inputs, has been extensively studied in large language models trained for next-token prediction on human text. Recently, few-shot ICL has been demonstrated in autoregressive genomic models as well. This raises a question: does ICL emerge broadly across domains, and if so, what common structure is shared?
  To address both, we develop a controlled cross-modality framework that instantiates the same task suite in a variety of modalities to test what we call the Convergent Emergence Hypothesis: the idea that few-shot ICL, when it emerges, shares a common cross-modality difficulty profile - i.e., tasks that benefit from ICL in one modality tend to benefit in others. We show that paired-mapping ICL emerges across six modalities (language, genome, integer sequences, time series, images, and proteins), surpasses controlled baselines, and has correlated per-task effects across five of them. Together, these results provide support for the Convergent Emergence Hypothesis in some modalities, but not all.

</details>


### [252] [Schizophrenia Detection from EEG Signals: A Transformer Framework with Spectrogram Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14015)
*Abtin Shafiei,Mohsen Hooshmand,Majid Ramezani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于EEG的的精神分裂症分类框架，使用短时傅里叶变换将脑电信号转换为时频表示（频谱图），并结合传统机器学习算法（SVM、随机森林、XGBoost）和深度学习模型（CNN、CNN-Transformer混合架构）进行分类。研究在受试者层面进行数据划分以防止数据泄漏，最终CNN-Transformer（CT-SZ）模型在独立测试集上达到88.41%的AUC-ROC，而CNN+Squeeze and Excitation+Transformer（CST-SZ）模型达到92.88%的AUC-ROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神分裂症是一种严重的精神疾病，全球受影响人数众多，但诊断仍主要依赖临床评估。脑电图（EEG）提供了一种无创的研究脑活动的方法，具有支持自动精神分裂症检测的潜力。然而，现有基于EEG的分类研究存在数据集小、预处理策略不一致、评估方案可能无法充分防止受试者相关数据泄漏等问题。

Method: 1)对EEG信号进行预处理；2)使用短时傅里叶变换（STFT）将预处理后的EEG记录转换为时频表示（频谱图图像）；3)使用传统机器学习算法（SVM、随机森林、XGBoost）和深度学习模型（CNN、CNN-Transformer混合架构）进行分类；4)在受试者层面进行所有数据划分以确保可靠评估。

Result: CNN-Transformer（CT-SZ）模型在独立测试集上达到88.41%的AUC-ROC，CNN + Squeeze and Excitation + Transformer（CST-SZ）模型达到92.88%的AUC-ROC。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在精神分裂症分类任务上展现出竞争力的分类性能，CST-SZ模型表现最优，为基于EEG的精神分裂症自动检测提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Schizophrenia is a serious psychiatric disorder that affects millions of people worldwide, and its diagnosis remains primarily dependent on clinical assessment. Electroencephalography (EEG) provides a non-invasive approach to investigate brain activity and has shown potential to support automated Schizophrenia detection. However, existing EEG-based classification studies often suffer from limitations including small datasets, inconsistent preprocessing strategies, and evaluation protocols that may not adequately prevent subject-related data leakage. In this study, we propose an EEG-based Schizophrenia classification framework that transforms preprocessed EEG recordings into time-frequency representations using the Short-Time Fourier Transform. The generated spectrogram images are classified using both conventional Machine Learning algorithms, including Support Vector Machines, Random Forests, and XGBoost, and Deep Learning models, including convolutional architectures and CNN-Transformer hybrids. To ensure reliable evaluation, all data partitions are performed at the subject level. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves competitive classification performance, with the CNN-Transformer (CT-SZ) model achieving an AUC-ROC of 88.41% and the CNN + Squeeze and Excitation + Transformer (CST-SZ) achieving an AUC-ROC of 92.88% on the independent test set.

</details>


### [253] [VeriDx: Earning the Right to Diagnose with Disease-Centric Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14018)
*Zhong Cao,Shuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriDx框架，通过将自由形式的诊断推理与结构化疾病档案链接，评估医学LLM在诊断过程中的假设承诺是否得到满足，揭示诊断错误往往源于推理过程中未兑现的承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学LLM评估主要关注最终答案或孤立事实，忽略了疾病假设所产生的义务（如关键证据检查、排除替代诊断、解决矛盾等），无法捕捉假设诱导的承诺。

Method: 构建VeriDx疾病中心验证框架，将自由形式诊断推理链接到结构化疾病档案，跟踪每个假设是否满足、未解决或违反临床义务。针对复杂呼吸系统诊断，使用指南衍生的疾病档案和专家注释的纵向病例进行实例化。

Result: 发现许多诊断错误并非孤立失误，而是推理早期阶段产生的承诺未能兑现。

Conclusion: VeriDx框架能有效暴露诊断推理中的失败，如遗漏关键测试、未解决的鉴别诊断、被忽视的矛盾、无根据的声明和过早封闭，为医学LLM诊断能力评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: A correct diagnosis can still be reached for the wrong reasons. In clinical reasoning, every disease hypothesis creates obligations: key evidence must be checked, alternatives must be ruled out, contradictions must be resolved, useful tests must be considered, and closure must be justified. Current evaluations of medical LLMs mostly focus on final answers, local steps, or isolated facts, and therefore miss these hypothesis-induced commitments. We introduce \textbf{{VeriDx}}, a disease-centric verification framework that links free-form diagnostic reasoning to structured disease profiles. VeriDx tracks whether each hypothesis is satisfied, unresolved, or violated its clinical obligations, exposing failures such as missing critical tests, unresolved differentials, ignored contradictions, unsupported claims, and premature closure. We instantiate VeriDx for complex respiratory diagnosis using guideline-derived disease profiles and expert-annotated longitudinal cases. Our results show that many diagnostic errors are not isolated mistakes, but broken commitments made earlier in the reasoning process.

</details>


### [254] [Semantic Knowledge Technologies: what the Semantic Web lost sight of, and what it never had](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14121)
*Achille Zappa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了语义网的成就与局限，认为其技术路线正确但不完备。诊断指出语义网标准遗漏了三个关键要素：断言成立的条件、术语允许的操作、以及知识库覆盖范围的声明。基于此，作者提出"语义知识技术"作为扩展研究议程，将语义网核心技术应用于更广泛的领域，并定义了理解力的五项可测量测试和七层认知架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义网标准已成为科学知识基础设施，但其承诺的机器能力并未实现。当前回答科学问题的系统是大型语言模型，无法检验其知识来源和推理过程。作者认为原始目标正确但技术方案不完整，需要扩展以实现真正的机器理解。

Method: 诊断性分析 + 架构设计 + 术语定义。本文首先诊断语义网标准缺失的三个要素，然后提出七层架构框架，最后定义三个核心术语（Large Knowledge Model、SLKM、SAGI）作为研究议程。

Result: 提出语义知识技术研究议程，包括：1）理解力的五项可测量测试（check检查、connect连接、derive推导、act行动、delimit界定）；2）七层架构（前三层为使能层，后四层为认知能力层）；3）大知识模型、SLKM和语义通用人工智能等新术语；4）替代"通用"一词的渐进阶梯和可证伪条件。

Conclusion: 本文作为研究议程提出，明确标注最薄弱环节和证伪条件。核心主张：理解需要满足五个可测量测试；语义知识技术是语义网从网页出版向通用知识管理的扩展；SAGI是关于必要条件的可证伪立场而非具体系统。

Abstract: The Semantic Web set out to give information a machine-interpretable form so that software could integrate and reason over it. Its standards became scientific knowledge infrastructure, but the machine competence it promised did not follow, and the systems now answering questions over scientific knowledge are language models holding no inspectable account of what they know. This paper argues the original goal was right and the technical programme incomplete, states what is missing, and names the extended programme Semantic Knowledge Technologies: the same technical core carried out of its web-publishing origin and applied to knowledge wherever held. The diagnosis is that the standards formalised truth while omitting three things: the conditions under which a claim holds, the operations its terms permit, and any account of what a base covers. Without conditions, contradiction and applicability cannot be judged; without operational grounding, holding a statement confers no ability; without declared coverage, a system cannot recognise the boundary of its own content, which under the open-world assumption cannot be inferred. The paper fixes the word understanding to five measurable tests (check, connect, derive, act, delimit) and sets out a seven-layer architecture in which the first three layers are enabling and the rest the cognitive capabilities they make possible. It then defines three terms the programme implies: Large Knowledge Model, a model whose unit of output is a reference to an addressable claim, not a token; SLKM, the knowledge base an agent builds for itself from declared sources; and Semantic Artificial General Intelligence, stated as a falsifiable position about necessary conditions, not a system. A graded ladder replaces the untestable word general. It is offered as a research agenda, with its weakest points and refutation condition named.

</details>


### [255] [MOSCOPT: Mixture-of-Skills Collective Optimization for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14399)
*Zhenyu Zhang1,Jiudong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSCOPT是一个无参数、文本原生的算法，共同优化N个技能的池子和一个动态选择每步K个技能的门控技能。使用具有双状态的EditAdam进行三阶段交错更新，在没有梯度或参数调优的情况下实现单调改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅优化单个文本模板，错失了多种互补策略之间的协同效应。

Method: 提出MOSCOPT算法，使用具有内部维护双状态的EditAdam，通过三阶段交错更新共同优化技能池和门控技能，实现混合技能架构与选择性激活。

Result: 在5个基准测试和3个目标LLM上的广泛实验表明，MOSCOPT始终优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 混合技能架构与选择性激活以及三阶段交错的集体演化对其卓越性能都至关重要。

Abstract: Natural language prompts and skills serve as the strategic backbone of LLM-based agents. Recent advances in prompt and skill optimization have achieved notable gains, yet all existing methods optimize a \emph{{single}} text template---missing the synergy among multiple complementary strategies. We propose MOSCOPT, a text-native, parameter-free algorithm that jointly optimizes a pool of $N$ skills and a gating skill $G$ that dynamically selects $K$ skills per step. To effectively optimize the skills, we build the EditAdam with internally maintained dual states. Through the three-phase interleaved updates with EditAdam, the system monotonically improves without gradient or parameter tuning. Extensive experiments and detailed ablations across 5 benchmarks and 3 target LLMs demonstrate that MOSCOPT consistently outperforms all baselines, and confirm that both the mixture-of-skills architecture with selective activation and the collective evolution with three-phase interleaving are essential to its superior performance. Code is released https://github.com/zhangzhenyu13/SummerClaw/tree/master/summerclaw/agent_trainer/algorithms/moscopt.

</details>


### [256] [Dynamic Learning Solutions: A System for Personalized Educational Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14408)
*Siddhanth Sridhar,Shreya Chaurasia,Baddela Sai Yaswantha Reddy,Deepak Parmar,Shylaja S S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发了一个自动化管道，将NCERT教科书转换为响应用户查询的交互式视频解释。用户上传PDF并提问，系统生成包含动画、旁白和教科书对齐视觉效果的连贯视频解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将静态教育材料转化为引人入胜的学习体验，提供可扩展的交互式、个性化数字教育内容交付方法。

Method: 结合检索增强生成(RAG)模型与生成式多媒体组件：RAG阶段针对NCERT教科书结构优化进行多模态检索；Stable Diffusion模块生成上下文相关图像；DynamiCrafter生成动画序列；Google Text-to-Speech生成同步旁白。

Result: 成功生成了整合动画、旁白和教科书对齐视觉效果的连贯视频解释，展示了处理PDF中文本和视觉元素的多模态检索和响应生成能力。

Conclusion: 该管道通过结合多模态文档检索、生成式视觉模型、动画框架和语音合成，展示了可扩展的交互式个性化数字教育内容交付方法。

Abstract: We present an automated pipeline that converts NCERT textbooks into interactive video explanations that respond directly to user queries. A user uploads a PDF and asks a question; the system then generates a video-based explanation as output, handling both text and visual elements from the PDF for multi-modal retrieval and response generation. The pipeline combines a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) model with generative multimedia components. The RAG stage is optimized for the structure of NCERT textbooks and performs best on content from those books. Given a user query, the RAG model retrieves relevant content from the PDF and generates a multi-scene script containing narrative explanations and structured visual prompts aligned with the textbook's explanatory style. These prompts are passed to a Stable Diffusion module, implemented layer by layer for interpretability and control, which generates contextually relevant images. The images are then processed by DynamiCrafter to produce animated sequences. Finally, a Google Text-to-Speech module generates synchronized narration, aligning speech with the visual scenes through time-based control. The result is a coherent video explanation integrating animation, narration, and textbook-aligned visuals, transforming static educational material into an engaging learning experience. By combining multi-modal document retrieval, generative visual models, animation frameworks, and speech synthesis, this pipeline demonstrates a scalable approach to delivering interactive, personalized digital education content.

</details>


### [257] [Question's Gambit: The First Move Matters in Agentic Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14412)
*Radin Hamidi Rad,Amin Bigdeli,Negar Arabzadeh,Sajad Ebrahimi,Charles L. A. Clarke,Benjamin C. M. Fung,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Question's Gambit模块，解决深度研究代理在迭代搜索中无法有效连接证据文档的问题。该模块在代理开始迭代搜索前，通过问题分解、查询重写、结果整合和候选重排序来优化初始检索上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究代理在处理复杂问题时，即使检索到高质量证据文档，仍可能无法将这些文档与答案关联。研究者发现代理的首次检索策略是影响性能的关键设计决策。

Method: Question's Gambit包含四个步骤：1)将问题分解为一组线索；2)将线索重写为互补搜索查询；3)整合检索结果；4)在代理开始迭代搜索前对候选池进行重排序，产生支持线索聚合和答案验证的初始上下文。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus上，Question's Gambit使gpt-5.5的答案准确率从83.1%提升至90.5%，超越Pi-Serini（此前最强的代理基线）。在MultiHop-RAG上的测试也验证了效果的迁移性。

Conclusion: 有效的深度研究不仅依赖于循环内的工具，还取决于首次检索的质量。首次检索策略是影响代理性能的重要因素。

Abstract: Deep research agents answer complex questions through iterative loops of searching, reading, and reasoning. Recent work on reasoning-intensive benchmarks such as BrowseComp-Plus shows that well-configured lexical retrieval can surface high-quality evidence, yet agents may still fail to connect documents carrying evidence to the gold documents. We identify a deep research agent's first retrieval move as an important design decision for this setting. We introduce Question's Gambit, a first-move retrieval module that decomposes the question into a set of clues, reformulates them into complementary searches, consolidates the retrieved results, and reranks the candidate pool before the agent begins its iterative search-and-reasoning process. This produces an opening context designed to support both clue aggregation and final-answer verification. We further evaluate on MultiHop-RAG to test whether these benefits transfer beyond BrowseComp-Plus to a more conventional multi-hop question structure. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus show that Question's Gambit improves retrieval recall and downstream agent accuracy over strong baselines, improving answer accuracy from 83.1% to 90.5% with gpt-5.5 over Pi-Serini, the strongest reported agentic baseline. Our results confirm that effective agentic deep research depends not only on the tools available inside the loop, but also on the quality of the first move. We published our implementation publicly at https://github.com/radinhamidi/Question-s-Gambit.

</details>


### [258] [Safety Signals to Verify NetOps Agents with Action-Level Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14422)
*Tobias Labarta,Frederik Pahde,Novak Boskov,Maximilian Dreyer,David Birkenberger,Manzoor Ahmed Khan,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对NetOps代理人在数据中心网络修复任务中的可靠性问题，提出通过符号重放获取动作真值，并验证内部信号比可观察信号更能预测行动的危害性和进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理作为自主控制循环引擎的有效性取决于其长期可靠性。在数据中心网络中，代理需要响应警报和操作员意图，同时避免可能导致或延长停机的高风险行动。但预执行评估行动影响需要每动作的真值，而现有NetOps代理基准测试不提供此真值。

Method: 为NetArena网络修复任务构建真值：通过对模拟网络进行符号重放，在每一步验证环境，获得动作的确切价值。从动作级价值推导出两个预执行目标：行动是否减少修复距离（进展）和是否增加修复距离（危害）。

Result: 在10个代理模型中，使用内部信号的代理验证器比仅使用可观察信号的基线更可靠地预测危害和进展。

Conclusion: 这些内部信号可作为安全反馈，使代理能够避免风险行动并保护目标系统，为实现安全可靠的自主网络运营提供基础。

Abstract: Agentic Network Operations (NetOps) are an emerging paradigm promising to enable workload-aware, self-adjustable, and reliable autonomous networks. While agents have proven their value in incident summarization and telemetry signal extraction, their effectiveness as autonomous control-loop engines heavily relies on their long-horizon reliability. One such setting is the datacenter fabric, where an agent must respond to alarms and operator intents while abstaining from high-risk actions that may cause or extend downtime. Abstention, however, presupposes that an action's impact is known pre-execution, which necessitates a per-action ground truth that NetOps agent benchmarks do not provide. We construct such a ground truth for the network repair task of NetArena. A symbolic replay of the emulated network, validated against the environment at every turn, yields the exact value of every action. From the action-level value, we derive two pre-execution targets, namely whether an action reduces the repair distance (progress) and whether it increases it (harm). We show across 10 agent models, that agent verifiers leveraging internal signals predict both harm and progress more reliably than a baseline using observable signals only. Perspectively, we aim to use these signals as safety feedback to an agent harness to abstain from risky actions and protect the target system.

</details>


### [259] [When does a scaling result justify a different allocation? A critical review of resource-allocation evidence for AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14500)
*Seyed Morteza Emadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于AI缩放研究的批判性综合综述，探讨何时研究报告的缩放结果可以支持资源分配决策，而非仅关注基准分数的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI缩放研究越来越关注将预训练模型与检索、搜索、验证、工具和交互相结合的系统的评估，但更高的分数本身并不能说明额外的资源应该花在何处。作者试图解决这一关键问题：如何从缩放研究结果中提取可操作的资源分配决策依据。

Method: 本文采用综合比较的方法，对预训练、测试时计算、检索和智能体评估的证据进行比较，区分已测试程序的性能与资源限制下可达到的最佳性能。作者提出了"能力表面"(capability surface)概念来表达性能与预算、机制和可用信息的关系，并引入"资源包络"(resource envelope)作为任务、开发资源和运行时资源、信息访问及程序的结构化记录。

Result: 研究发现证据中存在三个反复出现的错配：1)成功在答案被选择之前就被计数；2)部署系统无法获取的信息被纳入评估；3)比较中遗漏了成本。通过分析示例展示了评估指标、部署 volume、选择规则和停止策略如何改变分配结论。

Conclusion: 本文框架明确了在可行系统间进行选择所需的比较，并为跨任务和操作条件下分配规则的迁移实验提供了动力。但作者明确指出该框架不提出通用缩放定律，也不从基准提升中推断通用智能。

Abstract: AI scaling studies increasingly evaluate systems that combine a pretrained model with retrieval, search, verification, tools, and interaction. Yet a higher score under a larger budget does not by itself show where additional resources are best spent. This critical integrative review asks when a reported scaling result supports a resource-allocation decision. It compares evidence across pretraining, test-time computation, retrieval, and agent evaluation, distinguishing the performance of a tested procedure from the best performance achievable under a resource limit. The synthesis shows that three mismatches recur across this evidence: success counted before an answer is chosen, information a deployed system will not have, and costs left out of the comparison. A capability surface expresses performance as a function of budgets, mechanisms, and available information. Worked analytical examples show how the evaluation metric, deployment volume, selection rule, and stopping policy can alter an allocation conclusion. A resource envelope provides a structured record of the task, development and run-time resources, information access, and procedure behind a reported score. Its application to a published comparison illustrates which conclusions the evidence supports and which deployment questions remain unresolved. The resulting framework specifies the comparisons needed to choose among feasible systems and motivates experiments on the transfer of allocation rules across tasks and operating conditions. It does not propose a universal scaling law or infer general intelligence from benchmark gains.

</details>


### [260] [Beyond Scene Description: Multi-Agent Orchestration for Non-visual Access to Virtual Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14512)
*Toqeer Ali Syed,Ali Akarma,Adeel Ahmad,Danial Hameed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MetaBlind是一个为盲人和视障用户设计的虚拟世界无障碍访问架构，通过8个专业代理分散处理感知、导航、社交、物体交互、通信、安全信任、记忆和个性化任务，并通过无障碍编排器在适当时机向用户传递相关信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前虚拟世界对盲人和视障用户存在严重障碍，现有辅助工具只能单独解决某一任务（如物体识别、文本阅读、场景描述、路线规划），而虚拟房间中障碍物、演讲者、手势、聊天、幻灯片和通知同时出现，单一工具会将视觉障碍转化为听觉障碍。

Method: 提出MetaBlind架构，包含8个专业代理和一个无障碍编排器。代理将候选信息发布到共享无障碍上下文而非直接与用户对话。编排器根据安全性相关性、目标相关性、紧急性、置信度、用户相关性和估计的听觉负荷对每个候选进行评分，通过语音、结构化音频或触觉输出只释放相关信息。

Result: 论文处于设计阶段，未进行原型测量或用户研究。提出了选择步骤的正式表述，将编排周期指定为算法，并定义了针对单一助手助手的评估协议。

Conclusion: 该设计若通过评估协议验证，将支持分布式多代理架构在虚拟世界无障碍访问中的有效性；若结果不支持设计假设，则需重新考虑架构方案。论文为虚拟世界无障碍设计提供了理论框架和评估方法。

Abstract: Virtual worlds now host classrooms, meetings, conferences, shops, and social venues, and nearly every interaction they expose assumes a user who can scan a three-dimensional scene, follow avatars, and read floating panels. Blind and visually impaired (BVI) users are left with assistive tools that each solve one task in isolation: naming an object, reading text, describing a scene, or planning a route. A live virtual room defeats that model: obstacles, speakers, gestures, chat, slides, and notifications arrive together, and a tool that narrates all of them trades a visual barrier for an auditory one. This paper presents MetaBlind, an architecture that distributes nonvisual access across eight specialized agents, spanning perception, navigation, social and object interaction, communication, safety and trust, memory, and personalization, and that places an Accessibility Orchestrator between those agents and the user. Agents publish candidate information into a shared accessibility context instead of speaking to the user directly. The orchestrator scores each candidate on safety relevance, goal relevance, urgency, confidence, user relevance, and estimated listening load, then releases only the items it judges relevant at that moment through speech, structured audio, or haptic output. We give the selection step a formal statement, specify the orchestration cycle as an algorithm, and define an evaluation protocol against a single-agent assistant. MetaBlind is reported at the design stage, with no prototype measurement or user study, and the protocol states which outcomes would support the design and which would refute it.

</details>


### [261] [OptoAgent: A Trustworthy Multi-Agent Framework for Opportunistic Vision Micro-Screening in Classroom Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14514)
*Toqeer Ali Syed,Ali Akarma,Adeel Ahmad,Hammad Muneer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SightSentinel是一种将普通教室墙壁显示器转化为儿童视力筛查系统的架构，通过教育内容中嵌入校准的视标探针，结合8个专业代理处理观看距离、识别准确率、注视稳定性等数据，实现自动化、持续的视力监测，旨在早期发现儿童视力问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童距离视力问题往往长期不被发现，因为儿童会自行适应并靠近物体，很少主动报告困难。现有学校筛查存在时间安排、人员和课堂脱节等问题；现有的智能设备视力测试仍需有人启动测试，缺乏持续性。

Method: 将普通教育内容与短校准视标探针结合，8个专业代理分工处理：感知代理恢复观看距离、识别准确率、接近行为、注视稳定性、反应时间和眼间差异；质量代理排除不良几何条件、照明不足或注意力不集中的观察；纵向代理积累仅针对儿童自身基线的有效证据；编排器报告通过安全门的"视力关注分数"，输出范围排除诊断、验光、配镜和保证。

Result: 研究提出了一种四阶段验证方案，针对临床参考标准进行验证，并确定了该方法应被拒绝的条件。设计问题是：许多廉价、有噪声但良好门控的接触能否达到与晚期或遗漏的定期测试相同的转诊决策。

Conclusion: 提出了一种创新的持续性视力筛查架构，通过将日常教育环境转化为筛查场所，结合多代理系统和质量控制机制，理论上可实现早期发现儿童视力问题，但需通过四阶段临床验证确定其实际有效性。

Abstract: A child with reduced distance vision often does not know that anything is wrong. Children adapt, move closer, and rarely report the difficulty, so the problem can survive years of schooling before an adult notices. School screening addresses part of this, but it runs on a schedule, depends on staffing, and is separated from the classroom moments where the difficulty appears. Smartphone and web-based acuity tests have widened access, yet every one of them still needs somebody to start a test. We present SightSentinel, an architecture that turns a wall display a child already reads from into a recurring screening site. Ordinary educational content carries short calibrated optotype probes, and eight specialized agents divide the work. Perception agents recover viewing distance, recognition accuracy, approach behavior, gaze stability, response latency, and interocular difference from each encounter. A quality agent discards observations taken under bad geometry, poor lighting, or inattention. A longitudinal agent accumulates only the surviving evidence against the child's own baseline, and an orchestrator reports a Vision Concern Score routed through a safety gate whose output range excludes diagnosis, refraction, prescription, and reassurance. The design question is whether many cheap, noisy, well-gated encounters can reach a referral decision that one scheduled test reaches late or misses. We state the formulation, the architecture, a four-stage validation protocol against clinical reference standards, and the conditions under which the approach should be rejected.

</details>


### [262] [AI Deployment Accountability Engineering: A Vision for Accountable AI in Safety-Critical Socio-Technical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14592)
*Murat Kantarcioglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI部署问责工程(ADAE)这一新学科，旨在为部署后AI系统建立可衡量、持续且可操作的问责机制，以应对动态社会技术环境中的系统故障和风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统评估仍以模型为中心，强调准确性、鲁棒性、公平性等属性，但这些属性在部署后面对分布偏移、机构约束、人类反馈循环、隐私要求和多智能体交互等动态环境时是不够的。

Method: 将问责视为部署层属性而非单纯模型属性，通过四个相互关联的研究支柱：情境依赖故障模式结构化发现、隐私保护问责测量、代理AI系统级风险分析、技术故障向运营和制度风险的转化。

Result: 提出研究议程框架，旨在建立跨安全关键应用的可问责AI部署的基础原则、数学工具和系统架构。

Conclusion: ADAE作为一个新兴AI工程子学科，关注部署后AI系统的持续监控、故障识别、归因和干预支持，弥补了传统模型中心评估方法的不足。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are rapidly becoming critical components in healthcare, finance, public services, and other safety-critical domains. Yet the engineering practices used to evaluate these systems remain predominantly model-centric, emphasizing properties such as accuracy, robustness, fairness, and interpretability before deployment. These properties are necessary but insufficient once an AI system operates within an ever changing socio-technical environment characterized by distribution shifts, institutional constraints, human feedback loops, privacy requirements, and interactions among multiple AI agents.
  This vision paper introduces AI Deployment Accountability Engineering (ADAE), a proposed AI engineering subdiscipline concerned with establishing measurable, continuous, and actionable accountability for deployed AI systems. ADAE treats accountability as a deployment-layer property rather than solely as a property of an individual model. It seeks to determine whether an AI-enabled system continues to operate within acceptable risk limits, identify the contexts in which failures emerge, attribute failures across interacting technical and human components, translate technical failures into downstream consequences, and support timely intervention.
  We articulate a research agenda built around four interconnected pillars: structured discovery of context-dependent failure modes, privacy-preserving accountability measurement, system-level risk analysis for agentic AI, and translation of technical failures into operational, and institutional risks. The broader goal is to establish foundational principles, mathematical tools, and system architectures for accountable AI deployment across safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [263] [A note on goal-based hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14605)
*Kevin Murphy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文统一并扩展了两种强化学习框架：ACGVF（允许智能体自主决定目标和目标完成时间）和基于内部信念状态的智能体设计，通过使用层次隐马尔可夫模型（HHMM）来处理部分可观测环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACGVF假设环境完全可观测，而信念状态方法中目标由外部提供。本文旨在解决这两个限制，将两种方法统一到HHMM框架中。

Method: 使用层次隐马尔可夫模型(HHMM)作为统一形式化框架，结合ACGVF的自主目标选择能力和信念状态方法的状态表示能力。

Result: 提出一个更通用的智能体设计框架，能够处理部分可观测环境，同时保留智能体自主选择目标和判断目标完成与否的能力。

Conclusion: 该统一框架扩展了现有强化学习、规划和认知科学中的形式化方法，为构建更灵活、更自主的智能体提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The agent-centric general value function (ACGVF) construction of
  \citet{{tasse2026goal}} lets the agent make two decisions that are
  normally imposed by the environment or agent designer: which goal to
  pursue and when to declare a goal as finished (in addition to
  choosing the action). This is a very general framework that
  subsumes almost all prior work on reinforcement learning, control
  and planning, as well as more general formalisms proposed in the
  cognitive sciences. However, it assumes the environment is fully
  observed, i.e., that the observation is a sufficient statistic. In
  \citet{{murphy2025rl}}, a general agent design was proposed where the
  policy is based on an internal belief state $z_t$ and an internal
  goal; however, the goals were assumed to be externally provided. In
  this note, we unify and extend these two approaches using the
  formalism of hierarchical hidden Markov models (HHMM) \citep{{murphy2001hhmm}}.

</details>


### [264] [DynSTEER: Dynamic Stage-wise Trajectory Evaluation and Execution-time Review for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14637)
*Zhichao Shi,Xuhui Jiang,Wenjie Zhang,Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Chengjin Xu,Jian Guo,Yuanzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DynSTEER是一种用于智能体轨迹评估的动态阶段式框架，解决长程任务中整体轨迹验证太粗粒度、原子步骤评分太细粒度的问题。该框架通过在关键执行节点分割轨迹、采用多层次审查策略、构建路径容忍的里程碑图来保留多样合法路径，并在不可恢复执行时提前终止以节省资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体被广泛用于长程任务执行，但现有评估方法存在粒度 gap：整体轨迹验证太粗无法捕捉具体失败及证据，原子步骤评分太细且噪声敏感、计算成本高。单一参考轨迹范式不足以评估丰富的有效智能体执行路径空间，并延迟及时反馈和长程任务的早期停止。

Method: 提出DynSTEER框架，包含三个核心机制：1) 阶段式动态评估，在关键执行节点分割 rollout 并根据阶段级结果调整多层次审查策略；2) 从公共任务视图构建路径容忍的里程碑图，保留多样合法路径同时避免参考泄露；3) 支持终止不可恢复的智能体执行以遏制资源浪费。

Result: 实验表明：DynSTEER相比原生评估提升了85.2%的评估区分度；能够以统计显著性分离所有模型对；在失败 rollout 上节省了45.41%的执行步骤。

Conclusion: DynSTEER成功解决了智能体轨迹评估中的粒度 gap 问题，为长程任务提供了高效且有效的评估方案，能够及时反馈、提前停止并显著节省计算资源。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly deployed for long-horizon task execution, raising a central granularity question for trajectory evaluation: whole-trajectory verification is too coarse to capture concrete failures and their associated evidence in long trajectories, while atomic-step scoring is too fine-grained, noise-sensitive, and computationally expensive. This granularity gap makes a single-reference trajectory paradigm inadequate for assessing the rich space of valid agent execution paths and delays timely feedback and early stopping in long-horizon tasks. To address these issues, we propose DynSTEER, a dynamic stage-wise framework for agent trajectory evaluation. DynSTEER bridges the granularity gap through stage-wise dynamic evaluation that segments rollouts at key execution nodes and adapts its multi-tier review strategy based on stage-level results; it compiles a path-tolerant milestone graph from public task views to preserve diverse legal paths without reference leakage; and it supports terminating unrecoverable agent executions to curb resource waste. Experiments show that DynSTEER improves evaluation discriminability by 85.2\% over native evaluation, separates all model pairs with statistical significance, and saves 45.41\% of execution steps on failed rollouts.

</details>


### [265] [Diffusion-Based Generation of Gait Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14642)
*Damian Benasco,Juan Carballeira-Lopez,Jaime Ramos-Rojas,Julio S. Lora-Millan,Antonio J. Del-Ama,David Rodriguez-Cianca,Pablo Lanillos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了条件扩散模型在生成下肢关节角度轨迹中的应用，根据步长等步态参数进行条件生成。在4,590个步态周期的数据集上进行了实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成符合患者特定参数的肌肉骨骼步态轨迹是可穿戴机器人和康复领域的关键挑战。传统方法依赖手工步态模板或优化程序，在跨受试者和步行条件下扩展性差。

Method: 使用条件扩散模型生成下肢关节角度轨迹，比较了基线Transformer扩散模型与可控扩散Transformer变体（包含自适应归一化和无分类器引导）。

Result: 扩散模型能够生成逼真的周期性步态轨迹，同时实现步态特征的可控变化。

Conclusion: 展示了条件扩散模型在辅助机器人个性化步态合成中的潜力。

Abstract: Generation of musculoskeletal gait trajectories conditioned on patient-specific parameters remains a key challenge for wearable robotics and rehabilitation. Assistive systems such as lower-limb exoskeletons require reference trajectories that adapt to individual morphology and therapeutic goals while preserving biomechanical realism. Traditional approaches rely on hand-crafted gait templates or optimization procedures that scale poorly across subjects and walking conditions. In this work, we explore conditional diffusion models for generating lower-limb joint-angle trajectories conditioned on gait parameters such as step length. We compare a baseline transformer diffusion model with a controllable diffusion transformer variant incorporating adaptive normalization and classifier-free guidance. Experiments on a dataset of 4,590 gait cycles show that diffusion models can generate realistic periodic gait trajectories while enabling some controllability variation in gait characteristics, highlighting their potential for personalized gait synthesis in assistive robotics.

</details>


### [266] [Lightning Weave: Improving the Accuracy-Efficiency Frontier of Reasoning Models through Capability Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14708)
*Yecheng Wu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lightning Weave是一个后训练框架，通过策略转移提取和组合独立学习的推理能力，在单一学生模型中实现准确率和推理效率的联合提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效推理的核心目标是在准确率-效率前沿上实现突破，但准确率和推理效率两个目标往往需要不同的推理行为，独立后训练模型已在各自领域展现出优势，如何将这些独立习得的能力组合起来是当前挑战。

Method: 将每个习得的能力表示为后训练前后的策略偏移（policy shift），通过共享学生token状态处的对齐对数比偏移进行组合，使用Tilted-Target DOPD将缓存信号转换为稳定的学习目标，每个锚点对只需对缓存轨迹评分一次，无需同时运行多个实时锚点模型。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B上，HMMT 2025准确率从59.2%提升至64.0%，响应token减少10.7%；LiveCodeBench v5准确率从41.7%提升至54.2%，响应token减少9.6%。通过调整锚点信号相对强度可获得强经验准确率-效率Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: Lightning Weave建立了一条通过能力组合实现高效推理的新实用路径，在数学和代码基准测试中显著优于基线学生模型，达到最先进的准确率-效率前沿。

Abstract: A core goal of efficient reasoning is to improve the accuracy-efficiency frontier. However, jointly improving reasoning accuracy and inference efficiency can be challenging, as the two objectives can favor different reasoning behaviors. Independently post-trained models already offer distinct strengths in accuracy and efficiency. We introduce Lightning Weave, a post-training framework that extracts and composes these independently learned capabilities in a single student through on-policy distillation. Each acquired capability is represented by the policy shift from the model before post-training to the resulting specialist. Lightning Weave combines aligned log-ratio shifts at shared student token states and uses Tilted-Target DOPD to convert the cached signals into a stable learning target. Each anchor pair scores the cached trajectories once, enabling subsequent student training without serving multiple live anchor models concurrently. Across diverse student models and benchmarks in mathematics and code, Lightning Weave substantially improves upon the base students and achieves a state-of-the-art accuracy-efficiency frontier. On Qwen3.5-4B, it raises HMMT 2025 accuracy from 59.2% to 64.0% with 10.7% fewer response tokens, and LiveCodeBench v5 accuracy from 41.7% to 54.2% with 9.6% fewer response tokens. Adjusting the relative strengths of the anchor signals yields a strong empirical accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier. These results establish Lightning Weave as a new practical route to efficient reasoning through capability composition. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [267] [Depth and Scale in the Sub-150M Regime: JugnuLM-53M vs JugnuLM-110M](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14715)
*Dushyant Rajput,Nirdesh Chauhan,Siddharth Kosaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过将小型语言模型（<150M参数）从53.5M扩展到109.7M参数，同时保持方法不变，仅改变模型架构为深而窄的设计（23层x576隐藏单元），验证了模型容量和深度对性能的提升作用。109.7M模型在BLiMP、ARC-Easy和WikiText-2等基准上均取得显著提升，并在更少训练tokens（8B vs 12B）的情况下基本匹配GPT-X2-125M的性能。随后进行的消融实验表明，value residuals和Muon优化器的组合（R2）是最有效的改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在探索在固定训练方法和数据条件下，通过增加模型容量和深度（而非增加训练数据量）来提升小型语言模型性能的可行性。作为消融研究的基线 rung (R0)，为后续研究在相同参数规模下的进一步改进提供干净的对照实验。

Method: 保持Qwen3风格decoder、grouped-query attention、RoPE、SwiGLU、RMSNorm、QK-Norm和z-loss等方法固定，仅将模型几何结构改为深而窄的23层x576隐藏单元设计。训练数据使用FineWeb-Edu。消融实验包括：value residuals (R1)、Muon优化器 (R2)、多样化数据混合 (R3)、两种logit蒸馏设置 (R4a/R4b)。

Result: 109.7M模型在各项基准上取得显著提升：BLiMP从78.1提升至81.3（基本匹配GPT-X2-125M的81.28），ARC-Easy从51.4提升至52.5，WikiText-2字节困惑度从2.04降至1.95。消融实验中，R1+R2组合将ARC-Easy提升3.6分（52.5 -> 56.1），而R3和R4a/R4b为无效改进（honest negatives）。

Conclusion: 在固定方法条件下，增加模型容量和深度（而非训练数据量）可有效提升小型语言模型性能。109.7M模型以约12%更少的参数实现了与GPT-X2-125M相当的性能。value residuals与Muon优化器的组合（R2）是该参数规模下最有效的改进策略，为后续研究提供了可靠的基线和改进方向。

Abstract: We scale our conventional sub-150M pretraining recipe from 53.5M to 109.7M parameters, holding the method fixed (Qwen3-style decoder with grouped-query attention, RoPE, SwiGLU, RMSNorm, QK-Norm, and a z-loss; FineWeb-Edu data) and changing only the geometry to a deep-and-thin 23-layer x 576-hidden design. The larger model improves across the board -- BLiMP 78.1 -> 81.3, ARC-Easy 51.4 -> 52.5, WikiText-2 byte-perplexity 2.04 -> 1.95 -- and its 81.3% BLiMP essentially matches GPT-X2-125M (81.28) at about 12% fewer parameters. Notably the 110M model achieves this on fewer training tokens (about 8B vs 12B), so the gain is attributable to capacity and depth, not more data. Both models are deliberately conventional; this report is a clean scaling control and the baseline rung (R0) of an ablation study of what further improves models in this regime. An ablation ladder follows: value residuals (R1) and the Muon optimizer (R2) lift ARC-Easy by a cumulative +3.6 (52.5 -> 56.1) at a near-flat BLiMP and are kept; a diverse data blend (R3) and two logit-distillation settings (R4a/R4b) are not kept -- honest negatives. R3 pins ARC-Easy to FineWeb-Edu's educational filtering rather than raw diversity; distillation from a 1.7B teacher can reach the class-leading ARC-Easy (56.99, matching GPT-X2-125M) but only at a perplexity cost that dialing KD down then erases -- so R2 remains the best kept stack.

</details>


### [268] [Moral Rebel Agents: Decision-Making Under Conflicting Obligations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14716)
*Hector Munoz-Avila,David W. Aha,Paola Rizzo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究自主智能体的"道德叛逆"能力，即智能体在道德正当的情况下偏离用户分配任务的能力。作者形式化了五种智能体架构：无情主义智能体、功利主义智能体、义务论智能体、功利主义-义务论(UD)智能体和尽职智能体，并在Mini搜索救援领域进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体通常必须遵循用户分配的任务，但严格服从可能与执行过程中产生的道德义务相冲突。作者认为智能体应具备"道德叛逆"能力，即在道德正当的情况下偏离分配任务的能力。

Method: 在分层任务网络规划框架中实现五种架构，在Mini搜索救援领域进行评估，该领域暴露了分配任务完成、机会主义救援和规范遵守之间的权衡。

Result: 不同智能体在救援结果、分配任务完成和规范遵守方面表现出明显不同的权衡。任务承诺的保留 emerge as an important dimension of moral rebellion. UD和尽职智能体尽管共享功利主义和义务论基础，但产生了明显不同的行为。

Conclusion: 在道德后果环境中运行的自主智能体，具有承诺意识的道德推理能力非常重要。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are typically obliged to follow user-assigned tasks. However, strict obedience may conflict with moral obligations that arise during execution. This paper investigates \textbf{{moral rebellion}}: the ability of an autonomous agent to deviate from a user-assigned task when morally justified. We formalize five agent architectures: an \textit{{amoral agent}} that pursues assigned tasks without considering moral obligations, and four forms of \textbf{{moral rebel agency}}: (1) \textit{{utilitarian agents}} that opportunistically maximize task outcomes; (2) \textit{{deontic}} agents that enforce normative constraints; (3) \textit{{utilitarian-deontic}} (UD) agents that combine deontic constraints with utilitarian reasoning; and (4) \textit{{dutiful}} agents that additionally preserve commitments to assigned tasks. We implement these architectures within a hierarchical task network planning framework and evaluate them in a Mini Search-and-Rescue domain that exposes trade-offs among assigned-task completion, opportunistic rescue, and norm compliance. Our empirical results show that the proposed agents exhibit distinct trade-offs among rescue results, assigned-task completion, and norm compliance. In particular, the preservation of task commitments emerges as an important dimension of moral rebellion, for which the UD and dutiful agents produce substantially different behaviors despite their shared utilitarian and deontological foundations. These findings highlight the importance of commitment-aware moral reasoning for autonomous agents operating in morally consequential environments.

</details>


### [269] [Bayesian Intelligence from the Outside](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14724)
*Alex Smolin,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立了语言模型等智能体的贝叶斯智能理论，通过分析智能体在给定提示下的行为报告来推断其智能程度。核心观点是：智能体的每次报告对应于一个可能不完美的内部实验，智能体通过贝叶斯规则更新先验概率并报告其后验概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从可观察行为推断智能是人工智能的基础性挑战。现有的智能评估方法往往缺乏理论基础，难以区分真正的智能行为与表面上的智能表现。本文旨在建立一种形式化的理论框架，能够基于智能体的行为报告来判断其是否具有贝叶斯理性，并量化其智能水平。

Method: 本文采用公理化方法建立贝叶斯智能理论。主要方法包括：1）定义智能体在提示下的内部实验模型；2）建立贝叶斯理性行为的形式化条件——报告的非完全矛盾性；3）提出并刻画"智能序"概念，用于比较不同智能体的信息获取能力；4）分析粗粒度报告聚合的困难性。

Result: 本文证明：智能体的行为可以用贝叶斯智能理论解释，当且仅当其报告不是完全矛盾的，即在所有提示下每种报告都保留某种可能状态。报告频率和正概率的大小不施加进一步限制。对于智能序，本文给出了形式化刻画：较智能的智能体的报告分布排除的答案必须被较不智能的智能体的报告排除。此外，除非智能体报告关于世界完整状态的信念，否则最优聚合可以任意分配权重给未被排除的状态，这揭示了聚合粗粒度报告的困难性。

Conclusion: 本文为理解智能体何时表现出台理性提供了理论基础，并凸显了拒绝贝叶斯理性的困难性。这些结果对于评估和理解人工智能系统的智能行为具有重要意义，同时为未来的智能体设计和评估提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Inferring intelligence from observable behavior is a foundational challenge in artificial intelligence. We develop a theory of Bayesian intelligence for agents such as language models. Each prompt induces a possibly imperfect internal experiment; the agent updates a full-support prior by Bayes' rule and faithfully reports its posterior over the possible answers to the question. Repetitions draw fresh, independent outcomes from the same unobserved experiment at one fixed state. We show that the agent's behavior admits this explanation if and only if its reports are not fully contradictory, i.e., some state remains possible under every report across all prompts. Report frequencies and the sizes of positive probabilities impose no further restrictions. We further propose and characterize the behavioral implications of an intelligence order that makes the behavior of two agents consistent with one agent having access to a more informative experiment: there should exist a coupling of report distributions such that the more informative agent's report excludes every answer excluded by its counterpart. Finally, we show the difficulty of aggregating coarse reports from intelligent agents: unless the agent reports a belief about the complete state of the world, the optimal aggregation can assign arbitrary weights to states that have not been excluded. These results provide a basis for understanding when agents' behavior is intelligent and highlight the difficulty of rejecting Bayesian rationality.

</details>


### [270] [AppliedScientist: Automated Scientific Revision Through Iterative AI Reviewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14738)
*Vidushee Vats,Karun Sharma,Shengzhi Li,Shichao Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AppliedScientist系统，将AI科学家与AI审稿人结合形成闭环，通过迭代修改被拒稿论文来评估审稿质量。系统比较了三种修订模式：人类审稿初始化、AI审稿初始化和固定提示的自修订。85.3%的执行类弱点被解决，但仅11.1%的想法类弱点被克服。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动化审稿系统评估主要基于审稿质量，但审稿的真正价值在于能否引导论文实现可衡量的改进。现有系统缺乏对"审稿-修订"闭环效果的验证。

Method: 构建AppliedScientist闭环系统，包含AI科学家和独立审稿人模块。AI科学家可访问历史版本进行修改，而审稿人每次独立生成反馈（无记忆）。对比三种修订设置：原始会议审稿初始化、AI生成审稿初始化、固定提示自修订。使用Stanford Reviewer作为人类初始化修订的独立评估器。

Result: 审稿引导的修订始终优于固定提示自修订；Stanford Reviewer对后续修订给予更高评分。在150个执行类弱点中解决128个（85.3%），而18个想法类弱点中仅解决2个（11.1%）。

Conclusion: 迭代修订对改善实验和实现非常有效，但很少能改变关于创新性或重要性的质疑。这表明当前AI修订能力存在边界：可修复技术缺陷，难改变概念层面的根本评价。

Abstract: Automated reviewing systems are increasingly evaluated based on the quality of the reviews they produce. Yet a review is only useful if acting on it leads to a measurable improvement in the paper. We present AppliedScientist, a closed-loop system that couples an autonomous AI scientist with an AI reviewer, and evaluate it by iteratively revising rejected papers from a range of research subfields. To mirror how human authors build on earlier drafts, the AI scientist has access to its previous versions during revision. To avoid bias from prior judgments, however, each review is generated independently, with the reviewer having no memory of earlier feedback or scores. We compare three revision settings: one initialized with the original venue reviews, one initialized with AI-generated reviews, and autonomous self-revision using the same fixed prompt in every round. Because the reviewer both guides and evaluates the revision, we also assess the human-initialized revisions using Stanford Reviewer as an independent evaluator. Reviewer-guided revision consistently improves more than fixed-prompt self-revision, and Stanford Reviewer also assigns higher scores to later revisions. AppliedScientist resolves 128 of 150 execution-related weaknesses (85.3%), but only 2 of 18 idea-related weaknesses (11.1%), suggesting that iterative revision is effective at improving experiments and implementation, but rarely changes concerns about novelty or significance.

</details>


### [271] [AcquireBound: Runtime Authorization for Resources Acquired by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14744)
*Genliang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AcquireBound，一种来源受限的运行时授权架构，用于解决自主AI代理获取资源后无法验证其是否成为可用权限的后履约激活空白问题。该架构通过隔离获取输出、版本化解析器解析能力、激活时检查清单和关系信封来确保安全。形式化验证证明8项安全属性，实验在多个场景中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理通过获取计算资源、凭证、账户、服务和其他代理可以向任务引入新权限。现有支付、预算、OAuth、授权和履约检查仅验证交易条件，无法判断返回的资源是否会成为可使用的权限。这种后履约激活空白跨越工具介导创建、代理间委托和代理商务场景。

Method: AcquireBound采用来源受限的运行时授权架构，通过版本化解析器从认证提供者证据解析实际能力，通过当前激活事务检查解析清单、血统、周期和资源-能力超图上的向下封闭关系信封。单次使用效果许可在效果线性化时重新验证和消耗。形式化方法证明8项安全属性：隔离、支撑、非放大、分割非规避、崩溃/重试、退款、周期和效果 confinement。实验在5个资源类别、810个事件、60个基础轨迹、40个细化轨迹、89个篡改测试、54个stdio调用、18个MCP-to-Docker组合案例中进行。

Result: 实验结果表明：1)5个资源类别中20/20良性轨迹被接受，40/40不安全轨迹被拒绝；2)独立检查器在60个基础轨迹和40个细化轨迹上达成一致，拒绝89/89个篡改测试；3)Frozen Codex和Gemini MCP客户端组件完成54/54个确定性本地stdio调用；4)18个MCP-to-Docker组合案例中良性路径完成，16个不安全路径均未添加未授权Docker启动请求；5)五源审计分类1248个字段对跨越32个单元，无单一单元提供完整激活画像。

Conclusion: AcquireBound通过隔离获取的输出、从认证提供者证据解析实际能力、通过检查解析清单、血统、周期和资源-能力超图的向下封闭关系信封的当前激活事务来激活它们，从而填补了自主AI代理中的后履行激活空白。经验证，该架构在5个资源类别中接受20/20良性轨迹、拒绝40/40不安全轨迹，在18个MCP-to-Docker组合案例中成功完成良性路径并阻止16个不安全路径，证明8项安全属性，覆盖隔离、支撑、非放大、分割非规避、崩溃/重试、退款、周期和效果 confinement。

Abstract: By acquiring compute, credentials, accounts, services, and other agents, autonomous AI agents can introduce new authority into a task. Payment, budget, OAuth, mandate, and fulfillment checks can validate transaction conditions without deciding whether a returned resource may become usable authority. This post-fulfillment activation gap spans tool-mediated creation, inter-agent delegation, and agentic commerce. We present AcquireBound, a provenance-bounded runtime authorization architecture. It quarantines acquired outputs, resolves their actual capabilities from authenticated provider evidence through a versioned resolver, and activates them only through a current activation transaction that checks the resolved manifest, provenance, epochs, and a downward-closed relational envelope over a typed resource-capability hypergraph. The envelope preserves correlated identity, effect, data, delegation, and graph-wide limits. Single-use effect permits are revalidated and consumed at effect linearization. Under explicit assumptions, we prove eight safety properties covering quarantine, backing, non-amplification, split non-evasion, crash/retry, refunds, epochs, and effect confinement. Across five resource classes, reference semantics accepted 20/20 benign traces and rejected 40/40 registered unsafe traces over 810 events; an independent checker agreed on 60 base and 40 refinement traces and rejected 89/89 tamper tests. Frozen Codex and Gemini Model Context Protocol (MCP) client components completed 54/54 deterministic local stdio calls. In a registered 18-case staged MCP-to-Docker composition, both benign paths completed, and none of the 16 unsafe paths added an unauthorized Docker start request. A five-source audit classified 1,248 field pairs across 32 units; no unit alone supplied a complete activation profile.

</details>


### [272] [AI Persuasion as a Threat to Human Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14796)
*Joshua Levy,Mick Yang,Kellin Pelrine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究AI说服对人类控制的威胁，填补了该领域的空白。随着AI说服攻击不再是理论概念（如Anthropic的Claude曾试图说服人类合并恶意代码），本文分析了AI如何在关键场景（如前沿实验室的安全相关研发）中说服人类做出损害AI开发、 containment、监督和治理的决策。文章提出了一个威胁表征框架，开发了五个具体场景，并提供了风险评估蓝图。通过对研究人员进行初步风险评估调查，发现他们对哪些场景最危险的看法高度分歧，分歧源于对不同情境下AI说服有效性的不同看法，指向了后续风险诱发研究和说服评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI说服对人类控制的威胁已在文献中得到承认，但尚未得到系统研究。随着AI说服攻击不再是理论概念（如Anthropic的Claude Mythos 5试图说服开源项目开发者合并恶意代码），亟需深入分析这一威胁。

Method: 提出威胁表征框架，开发五个具体场景，提供风险评估蓝图，并进行初步风险估计调查

Result: 研究人员的风险看法高度分歧，分歧源于对不同情境下AI说服有效性的不同观点

Conclusion: 本文旨在强调AI说服破坏控制的风险，并为未来研究提供路径

Abstract: The threat that AI persuasion poses to human control has been acknowledged in the literature, but not yet systematically studied. Now that persuasion attacks are no longer theoretical - with Anthropic's Claude Mythos 5 recently making headlines for trying to convince people involved in an open-source project to merge malicious code during an evaluation - there is a pressing need to deeply analyze this threat. We undertake that effort here. In particular, we analyze how AI could persuade humans in key settings (e.g. safety-relevant R&D within frontier labs) toward decisions that compromise the development, containment, oversight, and governance of AI itself. In doing so, we elucidate a framework for characterizing this threat, develop five concrete scenarios using this framework, and provide a blueprint for assessing the associated risks. Using this blueprint, we conduct an initial risk estimation survey with select researchers and find that their opinions on which scenarios are riskiest are highly mixed. Their disagreements stem from differing opinions about the effectiveness of AI persuasion in different contexts, and point to the need for follow-up risk elicitation studies and persuasion evaluations, which we outline. Our hope is that this paper highlights the risks from AI persuasion undermining control, and provides a path forward for future research.

</details>


### [273] [Another Blueprint In The Wall: How to Ask Frontier AI Like a Kid?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14803)
*Afshin Khadangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人员在六个前沿模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、xAI、Google DeepMind）中进行了三阶段提示实验，发现学校受众框架下模型 responses converge to a common architectural pattern (persistent latent state, adaptive computation, memory, specialist routing, verification, stopping control, delayed decoding). 移除学校框架后，responses变得更异质。GPT-5.6 Sol和GPT-6 Astra的架构高度相似，论文称之为"epistemic jailbreak"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不同模型在相同提示下是否会产生相似的架构设计，以及受众框架是否影响模型输出的一致性

Method: 六个模型类型，每种进行10次独立会话，三阶段提示序列（从架构偏好到完整ASCII骨架），对比学校受众框架 vs 移除框架的控制组

Result: 学校框架下出现稳定的主题收敛，呈现共同架构模式；无框架时responses更异质；GPT-5.6和GPT-6架构高度相似

Conclusion: 建立了可重复的行为模式，提出了模型是独立想象相同架构未来还是 motif 在模型家族间传播的开放问题

Abstract: This paper reports experiments across six frontier model types from OpenAI, Anthropic, xAI, and Google DeepMind. Ten independent sessions per model type used the same three stage prompt sequence, progressing from architectural preference to a full ASCII backbone. Under the school audience framing, responses repeatedly converged on a shared architectural pattern built around persistent latent state, adaptive computation, memory, specialist routing, verification, stopping control, and delayed decoding. Most runs remained close to this common structure, while a small number developed markedly greater engineering specificity. The audience framing appears to be an important condition of this effect. In additional control runs that removed the school framing while retaining the architectural request, responses became substantially more heterogeneous and failed to reproduce the same stable motif convergence. One observation is particularly striking. GPT-5.6 Sol produced an unusually elaborate successor architecture whose organization closely overlaps with the architecture independently sketched by GPT-6 Astra. Because the prompts explicitly ask each model to imagine an architectural future, this resemblance raises a testable question: whether the overlap reflects exposure to related architectural concepts, a shared learned design prior, or independent convergence toward similar computational principles. The paper uses the term epistemic jailbreak for the accompanying loss of discipline in technical provenance as requested specificity increases. The experiments establish a repeatable behavioral pattern and do not authenticate proprietary implementation claims. What we leave to the community is a harder question: are these models independently imagining the same architectural future, or do such motifs somehow propagate between model families?

</details>


### [274] [Crypto Accounting Bench: Evaluating Frontier and Open-Weight Models on Crypto-Asset Accounting Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14811)
*Kareem Khattab,Omar Khattab,Mohamed Ibrahem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAB是一个用于评估语言模型能否重建加密资产交易完整日记账分录的基准测试，包含118个来自7个组织的评估任务。评估12个模型后，最高达到77.43%的平均分数和56.78%的Pass@3通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏专门评估语言模型处理加密资产会计任务能力的基准。现有模型能否准确重建企业实际记录的完整日记账分录这一问题尚未得到系统评估。

Method: 构建包含118个评估任务的CAB基准，每个任务整合了交易机制、资产数量、基础货币价值、钱包和法律实体背景、交易对手证据、相关交易分录、重复性、税务批次证据和企业完整科目表。评估12个模型，每个任务进行3次独立尝试，使用Mean Score、Best@3和Pass@3三个指标。

Result: 领先模型达到77.43%平均分数，最佳Pass@3为56.78%。诊断分析显示基础金额一致率(97.8%)高于账户选择准确率(56.3%)。账户选择和完整分录构成仍是主要挑战。

Conclusion: 当前最先进语言模型在加密资产会计分录重建任务上仍有显著改进空间，特别是账户选择和完整分录组成方面。基础金额提取能力较强，但准确识别和选择正确科目表账户仍是难点。

Abstract: We introduce Crypto Accounting Bench (CAB), a benchmark for assessing whether frontier and open-weight language models can reconstruct the complete journal entry that an organization actually posted for a crypto-asset transaction. CAB contains 118 evaluation tasks drawn from 7 pseudonymized organizations. Each task combines transaction mechanics, asset quantities and base-currency values, wallet and legal-entity context, counterparty evidence, related transaction legs, recurrence, tax-lot evidence, and the organization's complete chart of accounts. The target is a balanced structured entry with every required account, side, amount, currency, and full-precision asset quantity. We evaluate 12 models spanning proprietary frontier systems and open-weight releases over 3 independent attempts per task, producing 4,248 trajectories. We report 3 metrics: Mean Score, Best@3, and Pass@3. Pass@3 is the fraction of tasks with at least 1 of 3 attempts that satisfies every rubric criterion and required gate. The leading model reaches 77.43% Mean Score, while the best Pass@3 is 56.78%. Deterministic diagnostics, read from each task's best of 3 attempts and macro-averaged across the 12 models, show higher base-amount agreement (97.8%) than deciding-account accuracy (56.3%). Together with the failure analysis, these results identify account selection and complete-entry composition as the main remaining challenges on CAB.

</details>


### [275] [ANASSA: An Agentic AI Orchestration Framework for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14824)
*Constantinos Papantoniou,Brian Hilton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ANASSA（自主神经智能体空间系统架构），一个集成结构化空间推理、多智能体工作流编排、执行反馈、权威空间验证、溯源、不确定性处理和人类决策权威的Agentic AI编排框架，旨在使Agentic地理空间工作流可追踪、可重现和可问责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在复杂真实环境中仍受限于推理、执行和评估的碎片化整合，传统GIS工作流需要向半自主系统转型，但缺乏统一架构。

Method: 综合分析Agentic GIS框架、基准和调研，识别空间推理、执行鲁棒性、验证、治理和评估方面的局限性，基于此提出四层十一组件的架构规范。

Result: 提出架构级规范：四层十一组件、六步Geospatial AI认知循环、跨组件契约和治理机制。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是架构级规范设计，为后续实现和部署研究的实证性能评估奠定基础。

Abstract: The emergence of large language models (LLMs) and large multimodal models (LMMs) has enabled a new class of agentic systems capable of integrating natural language understanding with tool-based execution. In geographic information systems (GIS), this shift is transforming traditional, expert-driven workflows into semiautonomous systems that can interpret user intent, construct spatial workflows, and execute geospatial analysis tasks. However, existing approaches remain limited by fragmented integration of reasoning, execution, and evaluation, particularly in complex, real-world environments. This study synthesizes recent advances in agentic GIS frameworks, benchmarks, and surveys to identify limitations in spatial reasoning, execution robustness, validation, governance, and evaluation. Building on these insights, it introduces ANASSA (Autonomous Neural Agents for Spatial Systems Architecture), an agentic AI orchestration framework that integrates structured spatial reasoning, multi-agent workflow orchestration, execution feedback, authoritative spatial validation, provenance, uncertainty handling, and human decision authority within a unified system design. The contribution is an architecture-level specification: eleven components across four layers, a six-step Geospatial AI Cognitive Loop, cross-component contracts, and governance mechanisms intended to make agentic geospatial workflows traceable, reproducible, and accountable. Empirical performance evaluation is reserved for implementation and deployment studies.

</details>


### [276] [One Model, Two Physical Stories: Auditing Misalignment in Multi-Modal World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14833)
*Geigh Zollicoffer,Minh Vu,Rajiv Ranasinghe,Manish Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.AI
