Abstract: We prove that every configuration of four through eight points in three-dimensional space, with no four points coplanar, has a connected full geometric flip graph under $2 \leftrightarrow 3$ flips. Every point remains fixed and present throughout the sequence. The proof brings each tetrahedralization into placing form at a convex hull vertex: the tetrahedra not incident to that vertex fill the convex hull of the remaining points. This reduction allows us to establish connectivity by induction, using the connectivity of regular tetrahedralizations. The main geometric tool is radial projection, which turns the tetrahedra incident to a hull vertex into a planar triangulation. When this planar triangulation is regular, varying its lifting heights produces legal spatial flips that progressively shrink the region occupied by the incident tetrahedra and reach placing form. Planar lifting criteria and compatibility constraints between the projections at different hull vertices resolve the remaining small cases. For eight points, at most 35 flips are needed to reach placing form.

</details>


### [347] [Low-Dimensional Embeddings for Gaussian Kernels on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15179)
*Soumik Dutta,Kunal Dutta*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机傅里叶特征(RFF)在保持流形上高斯核距离方面的应用。证明了对于任意正可达维数为d的子流形M⊂ℝᴺ，需要约O((d²/ε²)(log N + log(1/(εδ))))个RFF即可保证所有成对高斯核距离的相对误差在(1±ε)以内，概率为1-δ。该结果仅对环境维数和流形参数（如体积和可达度）对数依赖，同时保持了1/ε²的欧几里得速率。此外还证明了在该嵌入下，持久同调被保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核是核方法中广泛使用的相似性度量，但计算大量点对的高斯核距离成本很高。Chen和Phillips (ALT 2017)证明了d维欧几里得球中点的情况，但实际数据通常来自低维流形而非整个欧氏空间，因此需要推广到更一般的流形设置。

Method: 利用随机傅里叶特征(RFF)技术，通过傅里叶变换近似高斯核，构建统一的相对误差嵌入定理。关键工具包括流形的可达性(reach)概念、局部坐标邻域分解以及概率分析。

Result: 得到以下关键结果：(1)对于任意正可达维数为d的子流形M⊂ℝᴺ，t=O((d/ε²)log(vol(M)²N^{{2d}}/(vol(B₁ᵈ(0))²rch(M)^{{2d}}ε^{{2d+1}}δ)))个RFF足以保持所有成对距离在(1±ε)因子内，概率为1-δ；(2)近似界为O((d²/ε²)(log N + log(1/(εδ))))；(3)拓扑后果：加权Čech和Ripsfiltration在(1±ε₌)意义上互为 interleaved，其中ε₌考虑了距离畸变和核权重近似。

Conclusion: 首次建立了任意正可达子流形上的统一相对误差嵌入定理，证明了RFF只需对数依赖环境维数和流形参数即可保持高斯核距离，且保持1/ε²的收敛速率。这为核方法在流形数据上的理论保证提供了基础，并进一步保证了持久同调在RFF嵌入下的稳定性。

Abstract: The Gaussian kernel is a widely used similarity measure underlying kernel methods such as kernel PCA and spectral clustering, but computing Gaussian kernel distances for many pairs of points can be expensive. Using Random Fourier Features (RFF), Chen and Phillips [ALT 2017] showed that for points in a $d$-dimensional Euclidean ball in ${{\mathbb R}}^N$, $t=Ω((d/\varepsilon^2)\log(dR/\varepsilon))$ features suffice to preserve all pairwise Gaussian kernel distances within a $(1\pm\varepsilon)$ factor with high probability. We establish a uniform relative-error embedding theorem for the more general setting of an arbitrary positive-reach submanifold $\mathcal M\subset{{\mathbb R}}^N$ of intrinsic dimension $d$. We show that $t=O((d/\varepsilon^2)\log(\operatorname{{vol}}(\mathcal M)^2N^{{2d}}/(\operatorname{{vol}}(B_1^d(0))^2\operatorname{{rch}}(\mathcal M)^{{2d}}\varepsilon^{{2d+1}}δ)))$, or approximately $O((d^2/\varepsilon^2)(\log N+\log(1/(\varepsilonδ))))$, RFFs suffice, with probability $1-δ$, to preserve the Gaussian kernel distance between every pair of manifold points up to relative error $\varepsilon$. Thus the bound depends only logarithmically on the ambient dimension and on manifold parameters such as volume and reach, while retaining the $1/\varepsilon^2$ Euclidean rate. We also prove a topological consequence: under the same RFF embedding, persistent homology is preserved in the sense that weighted Cech and Rips filtrations built from Gaussian kernel power distance are $(1\pm\varepsilon_\star)$-interleaved, where $\varepsilon_\star$ accounts for both distance distortion and kernel-weight approximation.

</details>


### [348] [Perfectly Guarding Straits: Exact Algorithms for Weak Visibility Polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15510)
*Shouvik Mondal,Udvas Das,Sasanka Roy*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种名为海峡守卫问题(Strait Guarding Problem, SGP)的新型问题，它是经典艺术画廊问题(AGP)的变体。SGP将守卫限制在一条边(基边)上，输入为弱可见多边形(WVP)，目标是放置最少数量的守卫来覆盖整个多边形。提出了Witness-Guard算法，能够在O((n + OPT·ρ)(log n + log OPT))时间内精确求解SGP。此外，还研究了顶点守卫版本，并证明了Θ(n log n)的下界。作为推论，还给出了地形守卫问题的线性时间完美守卫算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术画廊问题(AGP)是NP难问题，作者希望找到一种可以在多项式时间内精确求解的特殊情况。SGP通过将守卫限制在多边形的一条边上，将二维覆盖问题转化为一维问题，从而实现高效精确求解。

Method: 1. 证明了结构定理：基边上的守卫如果覆盖了多边形边界，则必然覆盖整个内部区域。2. 提出Witness-Guard算法，使用见证点集来推导最优解。3. 利用排序来证明顶点守卫版本的下界。

Result: 1. SGP可在O((n + OPT·ρ)(log n + log OPT))时间内精确求解，是输出敏感的。2. 顶点守卫版本具有Θ(n log n)的最优复杂度。3. 地形守卫问题(ATG)获得线性时间完美守卫算法，改进此前O(n² log n)的结果。4. 最小守卫高度问题可在O(nk + k² log k)时间内解决。

Conclusion: 通过将守卫限制在单一边上，SGP将二维覆盖问题转化为一维问题，实现了高效精确求解。这一方法不仅解决了SGP本身，还成功推导出地形守卫问题的多项式时间算法，显著改进了已有结果。

Abstract: The Art Gallery Problem (AGP) asks for the fewest guards that see all of a simple polygon. It is $\exists\mathbb{{R}}$-complete, hence NP-hard. We show that for a particular class of polygons, confining guards to a single edge makes AGP exactly and efficiently solvable. We call this the Strait Guarding Problem (SGP). Its input is a weak visibility polygon (WVP): a simple polygon where every point is seen from some point of one fixed edge, the base. SGP places the fewest guards on the base that jointly see the whole polygon. First, a structural fact: guards on the base edge that cover the boundary already cover the entire interior, turning a two-dimensional covering problem into a one-dimensional one. Our main result is the Witness-Guard Algorithm, which solves SGP exactly in $O((n + \mathrm{{OPT}} \cdot ρ)(\log n + \log \mathrm{{OPT}}))$ time, where $ρ$ is the number of reflex vertices in the WVP and OPT is the minimum number of guards. It is output-sensitive and certifies optimality by a witness set of size OPT derived from its output. We also study the guarding-the-vertex version and prove a tight $Θ(n \log n)$ bound, with the lower bound following from Sorting. As a corollary of SGP, we obtain two results for altitude terrain guarding (ATG), a special case that SGP generalizes. We give a linear-time perfect-guarding algorithm, improving the previous $O(n^2 \log n)$ bound of Daescu, Friedrichs, Malik, Polishchuk and Schmidt. We also resolve their problem on the minimum guarding altitude, in $O(nk + k^2 \log k)$ time, improving on the $O(k^2 λ_{{k-1}}(n) \log n)$ bound of Kang, Kim and Ahn.

</details>


### [349] [Multi-Stage NeRF for Efficient 3D Coronary Artery Reconstruction from Two Narrow-Angle Angiographic Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15550)
*Deyu Meng,Mojtaba Lashgari,Yiying Wang,Abhirup Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出NeCA++框架，一种多阶段自监督神经辐射场方法，用于从稀疏2D X射线冠脉造影重建3D血管，在仅两个窄角度投影的临床现实条件下实现优于现有方法的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中冠脉造影仅提供两个窄角度的2D投影，而现有基于学习的2D-3D重建方法通常需要宽角度覆盖或多视角，难以满足实际需求。

Method: 采用两阶段渐进式重建策略：第一阶段重建粗略3D血管表示并确定"活跃区域"，第二阶段在该区域内激活高分辨率表示恢复精细血管结构。引入两种血管特异性正则化：射线对齐约束减少投影歧义，双峰密度惩罚实现早期血管-背景分离。

Result: 在三个数据集(ImageCAS、ASOCA、Synthetic RCA)和四种角度配置上验证，相比现有最佳方法表现更优，尤其在窄角度临床设置下，重建时间仅58秒/例。

Conclusion: NeCA++能够有效应对稀疏视角下的3D冠脉血管重建难题，通过多阶段策略和血管特异性正则化在保证解剖合理性的同时恢复精细血管分支。

Abstract: X-ray coronary angiography is the clinical gold standard for coronary artery disease during real-time cardiac interventions, but provides only 2D projections of inherently 3D vessels. Existing learning-based 2D-to-3D reconstruction methods typically require wide angular coverage or multiple views, assumptions that are rarely satisfied in routine practice where only two projections with narrow angular separation are available. To address these challenges, we propose NeCA++, a multi-stage self-supervised neural radiance field (NeRF) framework tailored to clinically realistic acquisition constraints. The framework decomposes reconstruction into two stages that progressively refine spatial support and representation capacity. In the first stage, a coarse 3D representation of the vasculature is reconstructed, restricting the subsequent optimisation to regions with a higher likelihood of vessel presence, termed an active region. Afterward reconstruction is restricted to this region while higher-resolution representations are progressively activated to recover fine vascular details. This multi-stage strategy focuses learning on anatomically plausible regions, mitigates gradient dilution under extreme sparsity, and stabilises global topology before recovering fine vascular branches. Furthermore, two vessel-specific regularisations are introduced: a ray-aligned constraint to reduce projection-induced ambiguity, and a bimodal density penalty to enable early vessel-background separation. Extensive experiments across three datasets (ImageCAS, ASOCA, and Synthetic RCA) and four angular configurations demonstrate consistent superiority over state-of-the-art baselines, particularly under clinically realistic narrow-angle settings, while achieving reconstruction within 58 seconds per case.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [350] [Machine learning-assisted calibration of Agent-based Models: surrogate-based optimization with Genetic Algorithm and Particle Swarm Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13247)
*Duguma Yeshitla Habtemariam,Jihwan Lee*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究将代理辅助进化计算（SAEC）应用于ABM校准，在遗传算法（GA）和粒子群优化（PSO）中嵌入机器学习代理。每个迭代中，代理筛选候选值，模拟器仅验证前50%，减少模拟需求同时纠正代理误差。评估48种配置组合，两个优化器、五个代理、四个校准目标，在两个对比ABM上测试：Brock-Hommes资产定价模型和Island增长模型。相对于最强纯优化器基准，最佳ML辅助配置在Brock-Hommes上RMSE降低20.0%，Island上降低63.8%；计算时间分别减少32.1%和61.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABM校准困难是因为其目标景观是随机的、粗糙的，只能通过昂贵的黑箱模拟来评估。传统优化方法在处理这类问题时效率低下，需要大量模拟计算。

Method: 将代理辅助进化计算（SAEC）适配于ABM校准：在遗传算法（GA）和粒子群优化（PSO）中嵌入机器学习代理。代理在每次迭代中筛选候选值，模拟器仅验证排名前50%的候选，从而减少模拟需求同时纠正代理错误。评估48种配置组合（2个优化器×5个代理×4个校准目标），在两个对比ABM上测试：Brock-Hommes资产定价模型和Island增长模型。使用ANOVA和Dunnett事后检验进行统计分析。

Result: 最佳ML辅助配置相对于最强纯优化器基准：Brock-Hommes上RMSE降低20.0%，计算时间减少32.1%；Island上RMSE降低63.8%，计算时间减少61.1%。ANOVA确认每个代理在Brock-Hommes两个优化器下都显著节省时间，GA在Island上也显著。Island上没有任何代理在参数恢复准确性上与纯优化器基准显著不同。最佳代理-优化器-目标组合随ABM复杂性变化。

Conclusion: 代理辅助校准效果取决于具体ABM，不存在通用最佳配置。最佳结果来自于特定组合的优化，而非平均效应。在Brock-Hommes模型上所有代理都显著节省时间，Island模型上仅GA显著。ABM复杂性影响最佳代理-优化器-目标组合的选择。

Abstract: Calibrating an agent-based model (ABM) is difficult because its objective landscape is stochastic and rugged, and can be evaluated only through costly black-box simulations. This study adapts inner-loop surrogate-assisted evolutionary computation (SAEC) to ABM calibration by embedding a machine-learning surrogate within genetic algorithm (GA) and particle swarm optimisation (PSO). At each iteration, the surrogate screens the candidates and the simulator validates only the top 50%, reducing simulation demand while correcting surrogate errors. We evaluate a full factorial of 48 configurations combining two optimisers, five surrogates, and four calibration objectives on two contrasting ABMs, the Brock-Hommes asset-pricing model and the Island growth model. Parameter recovery is measured against pseudo-true values. Relative to the strongest pure-optimiser baseline, the best ML-assisted configurations reduce RMSE by 20.0% on Brock-Hommes and 63.8% on Island, while reducing computation time by 32.1% and 61.1%, respectively. ANOVA with Dunnett's post-hoc tests confirms significant time savings for every surrogate under both Brock-Hommes optimisers and under GA on Island. No surrogate differs significantly from the pure-optimiser baseline in parameter-recovery accuracy on Island; the reported accuracy gains are therefore best-configuration outcomes rather than average effects resolved at this sample size. The best surrogate-optimiser-objective combination changes with ABM complexity, indicating that surrogate-assisted calibration depends on the model and does not admit a universal default recipe.

</details>


### [351] [An Evolutionary Computation Framework for Multi-Agent Q-Learning with Mean-Field Environmental Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13253)
*Lichen Wang,Shijia Hua,Linjie Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究了在网络群体中，多智能体强化学习与环境动态耦合的模型，通过平均场近似描述群体Q值分布和环境状态的演化，并在不同网络结构上验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究个体适应、局部互动与不断变化的环境条件之间的相互作用，这是网络群体中多智能体强化学习的核心挑战。

Method: 构建耦合学习-环境模型，智能体在固定图上更新无状态Q值，群体平均行为驱动环境变量动态修改支付矩阵；采用一阶平均场闭包，推导出Q值群体分布的确定性传输方程，并与环境状态的投影离散更新耦合；在随机正则图、Erdős-Rényi图、Barabási-Albert图和随机几何图上进行有限网络蒙特卡罗模拟对比。

Result: 平均场系统成功复现了宏观层面的合作和环境轨迹；轨迹级均方根误差随群体规模和平均度增加而减小；环境反馈重塑了学习的行为价值排序；强化反馈可产生对初始学习偏差和资源水平的显著依赖；环境时间尺度也很重要，快速响应可使资源状态在学习适应前达到边界，而较慢的响应则保留行为学习与环境恢复的相互作用。

Conclusion: 提供了耦合强化学习和环境动力学的群体级描述，表征了平均场近似在测试网络和参数范围内的性能，为理解网络群体中学习与环境的相互作用提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning in networked populations is governed by the interaction between individual adaptation, local encounters, and changing environmental conditions. To study this interaction, we formulate a coupled learning--environment model in which agents update stateless $Q$-values on a fixed graph, while their population-average behavior drives an environmental variable that dynamically modifies the payoff matrix. Under a first-order mean-field closure, we derive a deterministic transport equation for the population distribution of $Q$-values and couple it with a projected discrete update for the environmental state. The resulting model is evaluated against finite-network Monte Carlo simulations on random regular, Erdős--Rényi, Barabási--Albert, and random geometric graphs. Across the tested parameter ranges, the mean-field system reproduces the main macroscopic cooperation and environmental trajectories, and the trajectory-level root-mean-square error generally decreases with population size and average degree. The analysis further shows that environmental feedback reshapes the learned action-value ordering, while reinforcing feedback can produce pronounced dependence on the initial learning bias and resource level. The environmental timescale also plays an important role: a rapid response can drive the resource state to a boundary before learning adapts, whereas a slower response preserves the interaction between behavioral learning and environmental recovery. These results provide a population-level description of coupled reinforcement learning and environmental dynamics and characterize the performance of the mean-field approximation within the tested network and parameter ranges.

</details>


### [352] [From Process Loss to Assembly Bonus: Human-Grounded Diagnosis of Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13261)
*Ala N. Tak,Teruhisa Misu,Kumar Akash,Zhaobo K. Zheng,Kevin H. Joo,Jonathan Gratch*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究比较了人类群体聊天与LLM群体在推理任务中的 deliberative 过程，发现两者在"组装奖金"结果上相似，但机制层面存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于LLM agents越来越多地用于协作问题解决和人类群体模拟，仅评估结果已不足够，需要了解它们是否通过类似人类的 deliberative 机制成功或失败。

Method: 将人类群体聊天与匹配的LLM deliberation痕迹进行比较，测试Wason风格的演绎推理，然后检验相同的过程特征是否泛化到类比、溯因和分析任务。

Result: 人类和LLM表现出相同的组装奖金不对称性：讨论改善平均成员比改善最佳初始成员更频繁。初始答案多样性解释了模型异质性的影响。主要差异在过程层面：LLM群体更频繁地跟随多数，表面化更少独特信息，更早收敛；正确的少数信号主要在早期重新表达时才能成功。

Conclusion: LLM群体可以重现人类deliberation的一些结果层面模式，但在生成组装奖金和过程损失的机制上存在分歧，这对群体模拟和人类-AI协作具有启示意义。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used for collaborative problem solving and human-group simulation. This makes outcome-only evaluation insufficient: if LLM groups are used as models of human groups, we need to know whether they succeed or fail through human-like deliberative mechanisms. We compare human group chats with matched LLM deliberation traces on Wason-style deductive reasoning, then test whether the same process signatures generalize to analogical, abductive, and analytical tasks. Humans and LLMs show the same assembly bonus asymmetry: discussion improves the average member more often than the best initial member. Initial-answer diversity accounts for the effect of model heterogeneity, increasing movement in both corrective and destructive directions. The main differences are process-level. Compared with humans, LLM groups follow majorities more often, surface less unique information, and converge earlier; correct minority signals succeed mainly when re-expressed early. Interventions motivated by human group-decision research yield modest improvements in collective outcomes, but do not remove the coordination bottleneck. Together, these results suggest that LLM groups can reproduce some outcome-level patterns of human deliberation while diverging in the mechanisms that generate assembly bonus and process loss, with implications for group simulation and human-AI collaboration.

</details>


### [353] [Conceptualization and experimentation of asset market with price manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13304)
*Pau Fonseca i Casas,Aarón Montero Montero*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究旨在通过多智能体模拟重现人类交易者的行为和心理过程，并使用形式化语言促进计算机、经济学和和心理学等不同领域专家之间的沟通交流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解交易者决策对资产价值影响的需求，以及改进不同学科专家之间复杂行为描述的沟通效率。

Method: 使用多智能体模拟模型中的虚拟交易者重现之前的市场操纵实验，采用形式化和图形化语言描述行为。

Result: 证明了在多智能体系统中重现复杂人类行为的可行性，以及形式化语言在简化经济过程复杂行为理解和验证方面的实用性。

Conclusion: 多智能体模拟是研究交易者行为的有效工具，形式化方法可促进跨学科协作与交流。

Abstract: The primary goal of this work is to reproduce the behavior of a human trader, detailing his or her psychological processes to understand the effects of his or her decisions on the final value of an asset. The second goal is to use a formal language to detail this behavior as a tool for improving and simplifying the communication between all actors involved in the project, such as specialists from disciplines as diverse as computing, economics and psychology. As a starting point, we use a paper that shows an experiment that analyzes the influence on other trader's behavior when an agent handler and a trading robot attempt to distort the market. This work reproduces this experiment, using virtual traders that belong to a multi-agent simulation model, showing the feasibility to reproduce complex human behaviors and showing the convenience of use formal and graphical languages to simplify the understanding and the validation of the complex behaviors involved in an economic process.

</details>


### [354] [Learning How Much to Collaborate: Difficulty-Aware Topology Selection for Multi-Agent Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13890)
*Yunsong Hong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体代码生成系统中通信拓扑的选择问题，提出难度感知的拓扑选择器DATS，根据问题难度动态选择最优拓扑，在代码生成和数学推理任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体系统为所有问题选择单一通信拓扑，但实际上不同难度的问题适合不同拓扑。层次化协作在简单问题上仅领先2.4分，但在困难问题上领先21.1分，表明需要根据问题难度动态选择拓扑。

Method: 评估5种拓扑在614个问题上的表现；提出DATS使用图网络预测各拓扑成功率，将5种拓扑视为连通性顺序节点而非独立标签；采用成本匹配协议比较方法。

Result: DATS达到77.7% pass@1，优于always-hierarchical的73.6%和最强学习基线的74.3%；在四种不同 backbone 上保持4.1分增益；数学推理任务上从2.5分扩大到20.9分差距；所有11个成对McNemar比较通过Holm-Bonferroni校正。

Conclusion: 根据问题难度动态选择通信拓扑是有效的；图网络方法优于扁平多标签头；成本感知路由很重要；该方法具有跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems for code generation are deployed with a single communication topology, chosen once for every problem. This is the wrong granularity. Evaluating five topologies on 614 problems from APPS, HumanEval+ and LiveCodeBench, we find that the advantage of hierarchical collaboration over a single agent grows from 2.4 points of pass@1 on the easiest third of problems to 21.1 points on the hardest third, while its token cost stays about ten times higher. We propose the Difficulty-Aware Topology Selector (DATS), which predicts each topology's probability of solving a problem and selects the one maximising predicted success minus cost. Its predictor is a graph network that treats the five topologies as nodes of a connectivity order rather than independent labels, worth 1.7 points over a flat multi-label head. Because the cost penalty is a single scalar recalibrable without retraining, routers compare at equal spend: under this budget-matched protocol six cost-aware methods span 21.6 percentage points, and two baselines leading DATS fall behind once calibrated to it. Fixed at 40% of the always-hierarchical cost, DATS reaches 77.7% pass@1 against 73.6% (always-hierarchical) and 74.3% (strongest learned competitor), all eleven pairwise McNemar comparisons surviving Holm-Bonferroni correction. The 4.1-point gain holds across four backbones spanning fourteen points of capability, and replacing the 39 interpretable features with a graph network or a pretrained encoder shifts accuracy by at most 1.3 points, never significantly. A cross-domain study on 400 mathematical reasoning problems reproduces the effect, the gap widening from 2.5 to 20.9 points.

</details>


### [355] [Loop-Back Authority in LLM Agent Teams: A Paired Experiment on Flat and Hierarchical Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14767)
*Burak Agachan,Max van Duijn,Amirhossein Zohrehvand*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究通过配对实验，固定五個LLM代理、角色、提示词、工具、模型和数据，仅改变组织层级结构（经理是否可退回 worker 输出进行修改），在商业智能报告任务中比较扁平组织与层级组织的表现。结果显示扁平组织在 Utility（d=0.42, p=0.009）和 Writing Clarity（d=0.34, p=0.030）上显著优于层级组织，古典组织理论的权威链接预测失败。层级报告的模糊表述多 53%，每次修改循环与写作清晰度下降 0.14 点相关，且层级组织中 Writer 的初稿与扁平组织报告无差异，质量差距产生于修改循环。监督层消耗 51.5% 更多的 token 但无质量提升。监督者仅在能验证时才有价值，当只能发表意见时成为负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古典组织理论预测权威链接能加速输出收敛，但 LLM 研究中的谄媚现象和思维退化现象预测权威批评会使输出变差。现有比较研究在整个框架层面变化任务，无法单独检验权威链接对开放式工作的影响。

Method: 配对实验设计：固定五個 LLM 代理、角色、提示词、工具、模型和数据，唯一变量是 Manager 是否可拒绝 worker 输出并要求修改。任务为商业智能报告生成，共 43 个配对产品和 86 次运行。由五个模型的评判小组和确定性规范检查评分。

Result: 扁平组织在 Utility（d=0.42, p=0.009）和 Writing Clarity（d=0.34, p=0.030）上显著优于层级组织。层级报告与扁平报告长度相同，但层级报告模糊表述（hedge）多 53%。每次修改循环与写作清晰度下降 0.14 点相关。层级组织中 Writer 的初稿与扁平报告无差异，质量差距产生于修改循环内部。规范准确性在两种组织中均达到天花板。监督层消耗 51.5% 更多 token 但无质量增益。

Conclusion: 古典组织理论的权威链接预测失败。层级组织中修改循环导致更多模糊表述和清晰度下降，监督者仅在能验证输出时才有价值，当只能发表意见时成为负担。生产框架应谨慎使用层级编排，除非有可验证的标准。

Abstract: Hierarchical orchestration, in which a Manager agent reviews worker output and can send it back for revision, is the default coordination pattern in production multi-agent LLM frameworks. Classical organizational theory predicts that the authority link speeds convergence on decisive output; work on sycophancy and Degeneration-of-Thought predicts that authoritative critique makes LLM output worse. Prior comparisons vary whole frameworks on tasks with checkable answers, leaving the authority link untested on open-ended work. We present a paired experiment that holds five LLM agents, their roles, prompts, tools, models, and data fixed and varies one link: whether the Manager may reject a worker's output and oblige a revision. Across 43 paired products and 86 runs of a business-intelligence reporting task, a five-model judge panel and a deterministic specification check score every report. The flat organization scores higher on Utility (d = 0.42, p = 0.009) and on Writing Clarity (d = 0.34, p = 0.030); the classical prediction fails. The reports are the same length, but hierarchical reports hedge 53% more, each revision loop is associated with a 0.14-point drop in Writing Clarity, and the hierarchical Writer's first draft is indistinguishable from the flat report: the gap opens inside the revision loop. Specification accuracy is at ceiling in both organizations, and the supervisory tier costs 51.5% more tokens for no quality gain. A supervisor pays for itself when it can verify and becomes a liability when it can only opine.

</details>
